| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_ABSTRACT_INKS_RETRACE_QUICKPRINT_SWAPS.md` | v7.5.x **four operator-requested Print Builder → Sketch changes (one landing).** **(1) Optimizer path flexibility — retrace along an existing bead:** instead of a pen-up, when the next shape starts a *brief length* back along an already-printed bead (same ink, same Z) the needle stays DOWN and retraces there; opt-in `Sketch.overlap_travel_enabled`/`_max_mm`/`_pause_pump`/`_speed_factor` (conditional serialize → legacy byte-identical when off) + `SketchShape.retrace_from` (optimizer-stamped). New `_retrace_polyline`; compiler tracks `prev_print_path` + emits the retrace (first-pass gated; `move_to(speed, pump_creep)`); optimizer post-pass `_stamp_segment_retraces`. **CRITICAL round-trip:** a PAUSED retrace advances the pump by a negligible `pump_creep` (floored `>1e-9`/segment) so Quick Print's `_travel_mask` (`dp<=1e-9 & moving`→travel) never re-splits it into a lift+pressure-relief — "pause not stop." **(2) Abstract inks (pump-agnostic sketch):** NEW `SketchInk{id,name,color}` + `Sketch.inks`/`_next_ink_id` (+`ensure_default_ink`/`ink_by_id`/`ink_order_index`/`add_ink`/`rebuild_inks_from_shapes`); `SketchShape.pump_index`→**`ink_id`** (legacy migrate `pump_index`→`ink_id`=pump+1, verified on real `config/prints/*.json`). The 3 trajectory pump columns become a PREVIEW artifact keyed by `ink_order_index%3`; the single-needle weld break keys on the TRUE `ink_id` (`prev_print_ink`) so mod-3-colliding inks still break; unlimited inks OK (sequential swaps = one ink at a time). New Sketch-page **Inks** manager card (add/rename/recolor/delete + reassign-on-delete) + per-shape **Ink** picker replacing P1/P2/P3. **(3) Quick-Print runtime ink mapping + full sequential swaps:** a sketch using ≥2 abstract inks gets a mapping table (abstract ink → configured ink→pump, colour-defaulted, validated) + a one-worker engine: `run_prep` once → per ink-group (`aspirate_ink` mapped pump → discrete `PrintManager` job the worker blocks on) → `run_post_clean` (waste→wash→buffer) between → `run_print_cleanup` once → end at safe Z; reuses existing `PickPlaceExecutor` primitives (NOT the legacy `PrintPlanExecutor`/`InkSwapStrategy`). `_ink_groups` recompiles per-group sub-sketches (preserves welds/retrace within a group). `_build_settings(pump=)`; `_PrintBridge.multi_done`; abort/button/status wired. Single-ink & non-sketch prints keep the legacy path. Deferred (v1): multi-group syringe-budget pre-flight + per-group flow-ceiling precision. **(4) Right context panel rework:** one outer `QScrollArea` + PINNED Save/Send; the sequence card drops its fixed-height nested scroll; each print section is a COLLAPSIBLE content-sized block (chevron + clickable title→select shapes + per-shape op rows) that grows to fit; per-section collapse persisted. **Designed via 2 Explore + 2 Plan agents; AskUserQuestion chose unlimited-inks / full-swaps / retrace-along-bead.** **Needs GUI/HW verification on ME3B V1** (assign abstract inks + panel colours/collapse; Overlap-travel retrace holds pressure with no relief; multi-ink Quick Print maps + swaps + ends at safe Z). Tests: `test_v75x_sketch_abstract_inks.py` (17) + `test_v75x_sketch_panel_rework.py` (4) + `test_v75x_sketch_overlap_travel.py` (18, incl. `_travel_mask` round-trip guard) + `test_v75x_quick_print_multi_ink.py` (15); sketch (257) + quick-print (137) + adjacent (83) suites green. |

Gates: XY, Safe-Z-when-ZP, camera+µm/px, feature saved, map exists. **New "Rosettes" tab** (index 3, after Plate Location; `_zauto_tab_index` 3→4 — only hard-coded index): rosette-well picker ("(scanned)" annotated) + "Scan rosette well" (`_ploc_scan_single_well(name, True)` — factored out of `_ploc_scan_well`) + **"Map sub-wells from saved scan…"** (re-opens the mapping from the persisted `plate#well` mosaic + stored shift/µm-px — no re-scan/motion) + status + own live feed started by `_on_workflow_tab_changed`. **Single-well mosaics overlay the plate mosaic:** `MosaicStore.composite_with_wells` pastes each `plate#well` image at its extent (proportional crop when clipped); "Well scans" toggles (default ON) on Plate Location + Jog; **purity guard** — `_ploc_overlay_img` stays the PLAIN plate image (what `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` re-persists), the composite exists only in the pushed pixmap; `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` also translates every well-entry extent (metadata-only) so everything moves as one. **"Mosaic + well" = circles only:** `JogWorkspaceView` overlay mode draws NO plate body and outline-only rings (green cal / yellow approx / blue current) for every entry INCLUDING sub-wells (`_well_radius_um(name)` resolves per-well diameters); "well" mode byte-identical. Implemented alongside a concurrent session's calibration.py edits (Pump Compliance tab) — reconciled. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1** (scan→auto-mapping main-wells-only lands correctly; Rosettes tab sub-wells land correctly — the shift bug fix; manual re-anchor saves the feature → auto re-anchor travels/matches/shifts; well-scan overlay + circles-only view). | `MEBP_v75x_SINGLE_WELL_MOSAIC_AND_GUIDED_MAPPING.md` | v7.5.x three operator requests on Calibration → Plate Location. **(1) Single-well mosaics first-class:** "Scan well…" generalized to ANY well (`_ploc_scan_well`; scan-start gates now snapshot+clear+re-arm the single-well flags (stale flags mis-routed a later full scan — pre-existing); anchors invalidated by ANY map-moving op (re-anchor/quick-reregister/re-derive/plate switch); 2–3 anchors upgrade the fit). | `MEBP_v75x_WELL_TYPE_PRESETS.md` | v7.5.x **well-type presets for rosette wells** (operator: when adding a well to a rosette, pick a standard vessel — e.g. a **0.1 mL PCR tube** — instead of typing raw geometry; build out custom presets over time). The needle-clearance mechanism the request describes ALREADY exists (v7.4.8): a sub-well's **rim height** (how far the tube top sits above the plate) flows `Well.rim_height_mm` → `WellPlate.max_rim_height_mm` → `app.py::_update_insert_clearance` (`plate_top_z + max_rim + 3 mm margin`) → `StageController.set_min_travel_z` → floors every `safe_travel_to`/`ensure_retracted_to` retract (polarity-safe) so the needle clears the tallest tube before travelling over/into it — **that safety path is unchanged**. This adds a preset **library** to set that geometry in one click. **Decisions (operator):** full preset library (builtin + user-savable, builtin/user shadow-by-id) · single opening diameter (taper deferred) · reuse the existing rim-above-top semantics (no clearance-math change). **New `SupportClasses/WellTypeStore.py`** — pure-JSON `WellType` (id, display_name, `diameter_mm`, `well_depth_mm`, `rim_height_mm`, optional `ink_z_mm`/`volume_uL`, `builtin`) + `WellTypeStore` (`reload`/`get`/`all`/`save_user`/`delete_user`, atomic writes, lazy `get_store()` singleton), a close mirror of `PlateTypeStore`; dirs `config/hardware/well_types/{builtin,user}` (user shadows builtin by id). Ships **`builtin/pcr-tube-0.1ml.json`** (Ø 6, depth 20, rim 20, 100 µL; rim is a tunable guess). **Model:** `PlateDesign.Well.well_type_id: Optional[str]` (design-time link to the preset), conditional-serialized in `_entity_to_dict` (emit only when set → byte-identical legacy) + read in `_entity_from_dict`; `WellInfo`/`compile()` untouched (runtime uses the stamped geometry). **GUI** (`gui/pages/hardware/plate_designer.py`, `_build_well_card` rosette block, `_edit_context is not None`): a **Well type** `QComboBox` (`(custom)` + all types) + **Save as well type…** + **Delete** (user types only); handlers `_apply_well_type` (stamps diameter/depth/rim, and ink_z only when the type prescribes one; `None`=custom clears the link keeping geometry; scene redraw + `QTimer.singleShot(0, …)` deferred panel rebuild to avoid deleting the combo mid-signal), `_save_current_as_well_type`, `_delete_selected_well_type`. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_well_type_presets.py` (18); rosette-flatten/plate-types/plate-designer regression (80) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_OVERLAP_SEQUENCE_AND_NEEDLE_MODE.md` | v7.5.x three operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(A) Closed-loop over-closure toggle:** `SketchShape.overlap_closure` (per-shape, closed outlines only; conditional serialize → legacy-identical) — the compiler continues the printed path PAST the seam by ~the needle **outer radius** (`od/2`, ½-bead fallback) via new `extend_closed_path(path, overshoot)` (appended per pass AFTER the weld-flip; ≤ one lap; never re-closes — intentionally ends past the seam), so the loop fully closes (otherwise the needle pushes ink aside on re-entry and it doesn't close). No-op on open/filled shapes. **(B) Print-sequence panel:** new `plan_print_sections(sketch, needle, single_needle)` returns the ordered list of colour-coded continuous **sections** (maximal runs printed as one bead) + the **moves** between them, MIRRORING the compiler grouping exactly (first-pass weld + far-end flip; last-pass end + overshoot as the exit; `break_before` = `move`/`ink_change`/`layer`); groups by OBJECT (layer 0). A persistent right-panel card renders it (section rows coloured by the pump→ink from `_channel_info` built from `config.pumps`, click→`canvas.select_indices`; break rows; summary). **(C) Single- vs multi-needle mode:** `Sketch.single_needle` (tri-state: None=AUTO→`needle.num_channels<=1`, else explicit; `is_single_needle(needle)`). Operator-chosen semantics (AskUserQuestion): a channel/ink change breaks the bead **only in single-needle mode** (the compiler tracks `prev_print_pump` — set on printing passes only — and refuses to weld across a channel change; move-only `no_print` passes never gate); multi keeps channels welded (legacy). `optimize_print_order` gained `needle`/`single_needle` (single mode groups same-channel shapes to cut ink swaps). Auto-detect checkbox on the sequence card, overridable. **Panel==compiled trajectory in both modes.** Latent fix: the page iterated `config.pumps` as a list (it's a dict) → never found a syringe; now dict-safe. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → skeptic verify: 5 confirmed / 1 rejected): fixed `prev_print_pump` set by move-only passes (HIGH), plan endpoint ignoring overlap overshoot (HIGH) + thick-outline last-pass end (MEDIUM); documented intra-object multi-pass lifts + odd-layer overlap direction as intentional layer-0/per-object approximations; rejected "extend_closed_path doesn't re-close" (intentional). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_overlap_sequence_needle_mode.py` (31); sketch + print-library/seam/quick-print/extrusion suites green (288). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CONTEXT_PANEL_PILLS_AND_CUSTOM_SECTIONS.md` | v7.5.x **left context panel becomes a pill-picker host + a composable Custom panel, and the "jog panel sometimes disappears in a workflow" bug is fixed** (operator). **Bug root cause:** the left box's **visibility** (`_navigate_to` → `UIFunctions.toggleLeftBox`, an *animation toggle* keyed on `isVisible()`) and its **content** (`_update_mode_context` from `sub_page_changed`, which never touched visibility) were decided by two paths that don't both run on every transition; `WorkflowsModePage` isn't a `ModePage`, so `_navigate_to` pointed the stack at a *placeholder* and the jog panel only mounted on a later `sub_page_changed` → order-dependent shown-but-empty / content-but-hidden. **Fix:** a **single global `ContextPanelHost`** (`gui/widgets/context_panel_host.py`) replaces the per-page left `_context_stack`, and **one `MainWindow._refresh_left_context()`** (called from BOTH `_navigate_to` and `_on_mode_sub_page_changed`; `_update_mode_context` deleted) mounts the current page's `get_context_widget()` in the host's native slot (reused `QScrollArea` wrappers keyed by widget id — never `deleteLater`) AND sets visibility **explicitly** (no toggle; exception in a delegate → treated as no-native, never vanishes). **Feature (operator chose view-picker + shared scope):** the host has a `SegmentedPillBar` — **(Jog)** = the page's native context widget, **(Custom)** = a shared `CustomContextPanel` (`gui/widgets/custom_context_panel.py`) the operator composes from **sections/cards** via `＋ Add section`: **live camera** (`CameraFeedView`; safe-start on show, never stops the shared feed), **syringe overview** (`PumpRack`, fill-dict from `jog_control._refresh_pump_panel`; ink accent `.color`), **positions** (new light `PositionReadoutCard` reusing `PositionBar` + `_update_position_displays` read logic; Z→user frame, pump→µL), **jog** (`StandardJogContextPanel`), **hardware info** — registry in `gui/widgets/context_sections.py` (`SectionContext` + `register_section`/`catalog`/`build_section`; each best-effort → a broken builder shows an error card). Layout is a **single shared, persisted, machine-level** thing — `SupportClasses/ContextPanelLayoutStore.py` (`config/context_panel_layout.json`; atomic tmp+`os.replace`, listeners, env `MEBP_CONTEXT_PANEL_DIR`, `known_types` prune) → same cards everywhere the box appears, survives restart. Live `on_status_update`/`on_motion_tick` forwarded app → host → Custom sections (native jog panel stays page-ticked → no double-drive). **Scope:** `in_scope = native context is not None` = exactly "everywhere the left box already appears" (Jog / Calibration / jog-capable Workflow tiles); Print Builder / Full Print inner / Fluorescence / Common Print Settings / picker return `None` and keep no box (unchanged) — so the `full_print` carve-out / `current_workflow_id()` from the draft plan proved unnecessary. Host persists the **requested** pill (`context_panel.active_view`) distinct from the *displayed* view so an availability-driven fallback can't clobber a saved "Jog" at startup; `context_panel.collapsed` remembers manual collapse. Close-x + top-bar toggle → `_toggle_left_context`; host added to `_propagate_hardware_config` fan-out. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_context_panel.py` (20; incl. `_refresh_left_context` invoked unbound against a fake `self` — full MainWindow boot is intractable headless); full-print/jog-nav/needle-location/plate-z-autocal/plate-location baselines green (121). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_START_POINT_AND_PATH_OPTIMIZE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions for **print continuity** ("a shape is placed down and the start/stop location is standard … not good for print continuity"). **(1) Manual per-shape print start/stop with snap-to-lines:** new `SketchShape.start_point: tuple\|None` (conditional serialize → byte-identical legacy when `None`) — a world-mm anchor for WHERE a shape begins printing. Compiler helper `reorder_path_to_start(path, start_xy, closed)` (open → reverse to the nearer endpoint; closed → roll the ring to the nearest vertex + re-close; deposited geometry unchanged, only the entry/exit/seam moves); `_shape_paths` is now a thin wrapper over `_shape_paths_raw` that reorders each **unfilled outline** pass to the start point (fills / `region` / `travel` never reordered). Canvas gains a **draggable green start marker** (`_effective_start_world`/`_resolve_start_on_shape`/`_start_marker_screen`/`_start_handle_at`/`_draw_start_marker`; offset outward from the shape so it never collides with the blue resize handles; hit-tested BEFORE resize handles); dragging it reuses the existing `_snap(raw, exclude=selected)` → **snaps to existing lines/vertices**, stored via a new `_apply_resize` `"start"` case; `clear_start_point`. Page shows a "Print start: custom/default" row + hint + "Reset start point" for outline shapes. A shape can now start exactly where the previous one ended → the existing compiler **weld** machinery fuses them (no pen-up). **(2) Auto path-optimize postprocess (operator chose FULL reorder):** `optimize_print_order(sketch)` (pure, returns a copy) partitions the shape list at user-placed `travel` retract points (**fixed breaks**) and per segment runs a **greedy nearest-neighbour with all-seed restarts** (≤ 14 shapes), choosing each shape's entry orientation (rotate loop / flip line) and stamping `start_point` to chain them into the fewest pen-up travels; preserves all print params. Canvas `optimize()` (undoable) + a "✨ Optimize print path" button on the Print-parameters card reporting "N → M travel(s)" via `count_discontinuities` (counts lift-to-max-Z groups − 1 — the `n_lifts` ground truth; immune to the zero-length coincident weld-node segment that fooled a first pump-flat-run metric). **Fills/regions kept fixed** in the optimizer v1 (ordered, not reoriented). Design-time only (no motion); embedded vector `Sketch` (Library "Edit in Sketch") round-trips the new field. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_start_point_and_optimize.py` (33); existing sketch suites (154) + print-library/seam/quick-print-travel (40) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_REANCHOR_MOSAIC_AND_CAMERA_ORIENTATION.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested plate-calibration changes. **(1) Re-anchor moves the mosaic + wells as ONE unit, no-travel.** The mosaic is scanned once per plate TYPE; when the plate is remounted the whole map is offset. "Re-anchor map" previously shifted only the **well centres**, leaving the **mosaic image** behind. Now `CalibrationPage._ploc_apply_global_translation` also calls new `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by(ex,ey)` (extracted from `_ploc_store_manual_align`, which is refactored to reuse it) — shifts the cached overlay extent, persists to `MosaicStore` (before the `calibration_data_changed` emit → the Jog page re-reads the shifted extent), re-pushes via `_ploc_set_overlay_image` (idempotent: resets the view user-shift + align sliders). Re-anchor is now **NO-TRAVEL**: `_ploc_reanchor_live_click` (step 1) captures the feature's true absolute µm from the LIVE click (`get_xy_position(cached=False)` + `pixel_to_stage_offset`), `_ploc_reanchor_overview` (step 2) reads the MAP position from an overview click (well-snap `_predict_well_xy` or Free-select zero-ref+zero), `E = live − map` (same sign as the old `feature_abs − target`), `_ploc_safe_goto` NO LONGER called, Safe-Z gate dropped (no motion); overview click handlers gate on `_ploc_reanchor_stage` so a stray click can't pollute the run queue. Robust to an arbitrarily large remount offset (the old travel-first flow only worked when the offset was < ~½ FOV). **(2) Calibrated camera-vs-stage rotation applied to the click mapping.** `CameraManager.pixel_to_stage_offset` ignored the camera's mount rotation (identity: image-right=+X, down=+Y) → a rotated microscope camera drove live-view clicks the wrong way. It now applies the calibrated `rotation_deg` as `(dx_um,dy_um)=µm/px·R(θ)·(dx_px,dy_px)` (standard CCW `R(θ)`); **at θ=0/None it reduces EXACTLY to the legacy identity** (no regression for aligned/uncalibrated cameras). The sign is DERIVED from `PixelCalibrationDialog.plus_column_direction_deg`'s documented model (`p=−scale·R(α)·m`, stored `θ=−α`, so click→stage inverse is `µm/px·R(θ)·p`) and locked by a round-trip test. **Only the click mapping changes** — the displayed feed, the raw frames, and the `MosaicBuilder` build (which places tiles by stage position with NO per-frame rotation, so a capture-level flip would scramble it) are all untouched; changing orientation does NOT invalidate a scanned mosaic. Reuses the existing measurement/storage/restore infra: a new **"Calibrate orientation (vs stage)…"** button + "Camera rotation vs stage: θ°" readout on the `objective_calibration_card` measures rotation via `PixelCalibrationDialog` (no µm/px recal), pushes `set_rotation_deg` live, persists per camera identity via new `CameraCalibrationStore.set_rotation`/`get_rotation` (rotation-only write preserving `um_per_px`/`image_correction`/`hw_controls` siblings) + syncs the fresh rotation into EVERY objective with a µm/px cal (mount rotation is a camera property, not per-objective); `hardware_setup._restore_calibration_for_slot` now restores `rotation_deg` **independent of µm/px**. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → independent verify): rotation-math + re-anchor-flow clean; 3 confirmed defects fixed — malformed-extent guard in `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` (IndexError → logged no-op), the all-objectives rotation sync above (an objective swap could push a STALE per-objective rotation over the fresh one), and pre-existing `ObjectiveCalibrationStore.set_calibration` no longer drops a stored `rotation_deg` on a µm/px-only update. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (calibrate orientation → plausible angle; live-view click maps the right way — else flip the one-line `R(θ)` sign; re-anchor a remounted plate → mosaic + wells shift together on Plate Location + Jog, no stage motion, persists on restart). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_reanchor_mosaic_and_camera_orientation.py` (21, incl. the sign-lock round-trip); objective-cal/camera-rotation/camera-cal-store (65) + image-correction/mosaic-orientation-remap/plate-orientation/plate-location-click-rim green; plate-mosaic 115/116 (the 1 fail is the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic`). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_RETRACT_POINT_AND_BACKTRACE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(1) Colour-coded retract-&-move point** ("a color coded point … that retracts and moves the needle"): new `"travel"` shape kind — a placeable pen-up marker (pink diamond + up-chevron, `Tool.TRAVEL`, `TRAVEL_HEX`). The compiler emits `lift_out(z_travel)` → move to its `cx,cy` at the retracted travel Z (deposits nothing, stays up); it **splits the toolpath into runs**. **(2) Back-trace any path** ("take the continuous collection of lines between the retract points and move the needle back through with a user defined offset" + follow-up "also … in the plane"): retrace a run **reversed**, offset in **Z-height** AND **in-plane** (perpendicular parallel curve), with an **extrude-on-return toggle** (2nd bead / move-only). **Model:** `SketchShape` gains `z_offset_mm` (per-shape print-height offset) + `no_print` (move-only), both serialized (defaults → byte-identical legacy). **Compiler:** per-shape print Z = `max(0, layer_z + z_offset_mm)` (floored at the plate bottom so a negative offset can't punch through), `do_print = not no_print`, `z_travel` clears the tallest offset. New module fns `offset_polyline(pts, distance, closed)` (per-vertex averaged-normal parallel offset, consecutive-duplicate-safe, clamped miter) + `backtrace_shape(shape, *, z_offset, xy_offset, print_on_return)` (reversed + offset copy; lines/polys/regions reverse points + in-plane offset, circle/ellipse/rect grow by the offset; accumulated Z clamped ±40 mm so chained back-traces can't run away). **Canvas** (`sketch_canvas.py`): `_place_travel`, `_run_indices(seed)` (maximal printing run bounded by travel points), `backtrace_run(seed, z_off, xy_off, print_on_return)` inserts reversed copies right after the run; travel cases in draw/hit/extent/move/transform/snap; `fit_view` scale clamped to 200 (tiny-span guard). **Page** (`print_builder_sketch.py`): retract tool button, travel-point props card, `_build_backtrace_card` (Z / in-plane offset spins + "Extrude on return" checkbox + button) shown for a single non-travel selection; `_backtrace_selected` confirms on a negative Z offset (collision nudge). **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → verify, 21/38 confirmed): 6 fixes (chained-Z clamp, offset-polyline dedup, negative-Z confirm, fit-view zoom clamp, explicit travel no-ops); two HIGH flags proven non-bugs + locked with tests (multi-layer serpentine only flips travel *direction* not deposited geometry; travel-as-first-shape starts retracted at z_travel, and the `build_well_plate_job` preamble retracts anyway). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_retract_and_backtrace.py` (43); sketch/print-library/quick-print/multi-object-seam/print-setup-routine suites green (193). |
| `MEBP_v75x_EXTRUSION_VOLUME_CALC_AUDIT.md` | v7.5.x **audit + fixes for "27 G needs ~7× extrusion to print anything"** (operator asked for a full print-volume calculation check). **Verdict: the core arithmetic is correct — no units bug / hidden factor.** The whole Quick Print chain deposits exactly `bore_area × modifier` µL per mm (`flow = pct·area·xy_max·mod`, `speed = pct·xy_max` → `vol/mm = flow/speed = area·mod`; `move_pump_uL` µL→plunger-mm is clean; the Hagen–Poiseuille flow ceiling for a 27 G at water is ~376 µL/s, far above any print flow, so the clamp isn't limiting). Needing a multiplier is BY DESIGN — the 1× model = a filament the exact diameter of the bore = the theoretical minimum; a visible/spreading bead + prime + compliance need more. Four findings compounded it; operator chose to fix **A + C + E** (D folded into A). **(C) drop-gate under-extrusion (real defect):** `PrintManager._execute_print_path` skipped any segment whose `volume_uL ≤ 0.001` (at 27 G 1× = every segment < 0.029 mm) and `ZPStage.move_relative` drops `<1e-4 mm` plunger deltas — NEITHER accumulated → finely-sampled paths lost volume, worst at low extrusion (→ operator over-cranks the modifier). Fix: accumulate a `_pending_pump_uL` residual across segments, emit once it crosses `_PATH_PUMP_EMIT_MIN_UL` (0.001 µL), flush the tail at path end; pacing/logging still use the nominal per-segment volume (timing unchanged). **(A) two inconsistent bead models unified:** the Sketch compiler used `id_mm × layer_height × mult` (rectangle, layer-height-dependent) while FlowPhysics/GeometryEngine/Quick Print use `π·(id/2)² × mult` (bore area) — CLAUDE.md F-1 claimed "ONE bead model everywhere" but Sketch was never migrated. `SketchTrajectory.compile_to_trajectory` now uses the canonical **bore-area × mult** when a needle is supplied (falls back to `bead × layer_height × mult` only when no needle); its shaded thickness band stays a display WIDTH (`id × mult`) — width vs area differ by design, same as Quick Print. **(D) stale GeometryEngine module docstring** ("flow_rate = travel_speed × needle_OD × layer_height") updated to the bore-area model. **(E) modifier had no effect on the parametric/trajectory path:** `PrintObject.extrusion_modifier` existed but `generate_object_trajectory` never read it (called `compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` at 1.0) and there was no UI → Print Builder parametric objects / Full Print ran locked at 1×. Fix: `generate_object_trajectory` reads `obj.extrusion_modifier` and threads it through the 11 internal builders → the volume functions (all new params default 1.0 = byte-identical legacy; explicit `outer_ring_volume_uL` stays an absolute target since the modifier cancels in its ratio); a new **"Extrusion ×"** spin in the Print Builder object designer captured into `params["extrusion_modifier"]`, lifted onto the field in `_build_print_object` + the collection-commit `PrintObject`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-tune the 27 G multiplier — fine-path drops fixed + parametric path now honors the modifier, so it should need a smaller value). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_extrusion_volume_calc.py` (14) + updated `test_v75x_sketch_extrusion_thickness.py`; extrusion/sketch/quick-print/seam/planner-barrier/print-setup/full-print/library suites green (294). Pre-existing unrelated failures: `test_v73_trajectory_planner.TestFullPlanGeneration` (6, MagicMock `<` in `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._move_z`, untouched by this change). |
| `MEBP_v75x_JOG_TRAVEL_OFF_GUI_THREAD.md` | v7.5.x **fix — "program freezes when I jog to another well with the needle DOWN, but not if I hit Move-to-Safe-Z first"** (operator; suspected a safety-check bug). **Root cause (4-lens investigation + adversarial verify + `logs/app.log`):** NOT a broken safety check and NOT a deadlock — the Jog page's click-to-travel handlers called `StageController.safe_travel_to(...)` **synchronously on the Qt GUI thread**; step-1 Z-retract-and-confirm (`flush_moves` M400 + `wait_for_z_arrival`) takes **11–14 s** on ME3B V1 (up to ~60–120 s on a stall), freezing the event loop the whole time. "Move to Safe Z" first is fire-and-forget (`move_z_absolute` returns instantly), so the subsequent click's step-1 retract is a fast no-op → no freeze. The workflow pages (Quick Print pre-position, pick-&-place executors) already ran `safe_travel_to` off-thread; the Jog page + the workflow-page CLICK handlers were the outliers. **Fix (operator chose full + serial hardening):** new reusable `gui/widgets/safe_travel_worker.py::SafeTravelWorker(QObject)` runs `safe_travel_to` on a daemon thread, marshals `finished(bool)` back via a queued Signal, **busy-guard** so re-clicks are ignored (not queued behind each other on the shared serial channel). Wired into all 5 click sites: `jog_control` (workspace click + right-click fast-travel; + WaitCursor cue), `standard_jog_context._absolute_goto` (Absolute Go-To; Go button → "Travelling…"), and `spheroid_pickup`/`cell_targeting`/`cell_labeling` (both click handlers). Retract-before-XY safety **unchanged** (same `safe_travel_to` args; only *where the blocking wait runs* changed). **Serial hardening:** `ZPStage._read_until_ok` gains an absolute wall-clock cap `READ_OK_HARD_CAP_S=180 s` (`hard_deadline = now + max(ok_timeout, cap)`, top-of-loop check → outcome `hard_timeout`) so a never-completing move — whose Marlin `busy` keep-alives used to reset the wait window forever — fails after a generous ceiling instead of hanging the worker thread (and every ZP thread behind `_serial_lock`) indefinitely; `flush_moves` (own bounded loop) unaffected. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_travel_off_gui_thread.py` (8) + updated `test_v75x_z_retract_before_xy_travel.py` (join the worker thread); jog-nav/workflow-settings/ZP-serial/gentle-Z/safe-Z/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/stress green. Pre-existing unrelated failure: `test_v75x_zp_auto_reconnect_and_fast_z::test_no_z_max_keeps_defaults` (uncommitted `get_max_z_feedrate_mm_min` WIP in StageController.py, not this change). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_EDIT_SAVED_PRINTS.md` | v7.5.x **edit any saved print from the Library, back in the Sketch editor** (operator: "go into all of the print sketches overview and edit any of the prints"). A bake isn't losslessly reversible, so: sketch-created prints now **embed their vector `Sketch`** in the baked object params (`params["sketch"]`, added on BOTH save paths) → reload losslessly; prints without one (older/image/CSV) import **best-effort** — `SketchTrajectory.regions_from_trajectory` splits the baked Nx7 toolpath into printing sub-paths (pump-delta; travel dropped) → editable `region` shapes. **Library:** `PrintCard` gains an **"✏ Edit in Sketch"** button + double-click → `edit_requested` → `PrintLibraryPage.edit_print_requested`; `_object_trajectory`→public `object_trajectory`. **Sketch page:** `load_print_for_edit(name, prints_dir=None)` (`_sketch_from_stored` lossless / `_sketch_from_trajectory` best-effort) + `_begin_edit`/`_editing_name`/`_editing_stem`/`_prints_dir`; `_compile_for_export`+`_do_bake` (embeds sketch, threads prints_dir, `overwrite`) shared by `_send_to_print_setup` (new) and new **`_save_changes`** (overwrite in place, `print_file_saved` signal); **"New sketch"** toolbar action leaves edit mode. **`PrintFileManager.save_trajectory_as_print_object(overwrite=False)`** — `True` uses the base_name stem verbatim + replaces. Wired: `print_builder` (edit→load+switch_to_sketch; saved→library.refresh), `app.py` (`print_file_saved`→combo refresh). **Adversarially reviewed** (3-dim workflow → verify); 3 real issues fixed: best-effort now **recovers print height** from the toolpath (`zref_to_plate_relative`) instead of resetting to 0.2 mm; overwrite targets the **resolved stem** (no orphan when stem≠sanitize(name)); **edit mode is exitable** ("New sketch") + Send-as-new **retargets** the edit (no stale-Save overwrite). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_edit_print.py` (19); sketch/library/quick-print/migration suites green (130). |

| `MEBP_v75x_GENTLE_Z_LIFT_AND_DESCENT.md` | v7.5.x **gentle Z near the plate — slow lift-off + slow descent, standard for ALL workflows + user-controllable** (operator: Quick Print "seemed not to" do the slow lift; also want the last 1 mm of re-entry slow; make both standard everywhere + tunable). **Investigation (empirical Z-trace + adversarial verify):** the slow LIFT already fires in Quick Print (all lifts funnel `ensure_retracted_to`/`safe_travel_to`→`_retract_z_slow_then_fast`, first 1 mm @ 60 mm/min); it *looked* absent because a single continuous shape has no mid-print lift (only the post-print retract). The DESCENT was NOT gentle (single `move_z_absolute` at insert feedrate). **Fix:** new emit-only `StageController._descend_z_moves_only` (polarity-safe descent twin of the retract helper — fast to `target_h+slow_dist`, final `slow_dist` slow; ascent/short/unknown degrade to a single move) + `emit_descent_moves` (cached-Z read) + `set_descend_slow_final` + `_descend_slow_dist_mm`/`_descend_slow_feedrate` (1 mm @ 60 mm/min). Wired into `safe_travel_to` step 3 (covers pick&place / cell / calibration / needle-goto) and the discrete `MOVE_Z` handler (Quick Print / discrete) — **emit-only**, so both keep their existing M400+`wait_for_z_arrival` confirm and the `MOVE_Z` abort-before-extrusion (the review showed a confirming method would break 5 tests + silence the abort); `MOVE_Z` falls back to a single move on a mock/older ctrl via an `isinstance(_descend_slow_dist_mm,(int,float))` gate. **User control:** `HardwareConfig.gentle_z_slow_dist_mm`/`gentle_z_slow_speed_mm_s` (serialized) → `CommonPrintSettings` globals → a "Gentle Z near the plate" card on the Common Print Settings page; one distance + one mm/s speed drive BOTH lift and descent (identical defaults); `set_hardware_config` pushes them to `set_retract_slow_lift` + `set_descend_slow_final` (dist 0 = single-speed/legacy). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_gentle_descent_slow_final.py` (18); gentle-retract/zp-feedrate/print-setup-routine/always-safe-z/z-retract/quick-print-travel-split/quick-print-pick&place/common-print-settings/pump-settle/simple-pm/multi-object-seam/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling green (279). |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_TRAVEL_SPLIT.md` (addendum 2026-07-01) | v7.5.x **fix — connected line segments printed discontinuously (needle lifted at every shared node).** The sketch compiler welds connected paths, but at an *exact* shared node it emits a zero-distance, pump-flat coincident waypoint; `_travel_mask` flagged any pump-flat segment as travel → split → lift mid-stroke. Fix: a travel now requires pump-flat **AND** needle motion (`& (seg_dist > 1e-6)` on the pump branch) so weld/coincident nodes stay continuous while genuine separate-shape travels still split. | `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_DISENGAGE_AND_SINK_TIMING.md` | v7.5.x **Spheroid Pick & Place: optional disengagement extra-aspirate + calibrated sink-timing model + guided calibration** (operator: a spheroid rises up the bore then SINKS; if it sinks before the needle leaves the well it's lost, if aspirated too far up it gets stuck, and at placement they want it just at the tip for minimal excess; also sometimes needs extra flow to pop it off the glass). **Model:** `height_mm = V_uL / bore_area_mm2` (bore = `NeedleSpec.cross_section_area_mm2`). Calibration builds a **timing curve `t_sink(lift)`** via a **cumulative staircase** (aspirate increasing volumes on one test spheroid; operator clicks "Visible at tip" each time it sinks back → sample `(lift=ΔV/area, sink_s)`). At run time (per move) the executor **inverts** the curve so `sink_time ≈ travel_time + margin`, sizing the aspirate to `max(carrier, timing_vol)` (**floor at the sphere-capture volume**) and waiting only the leftover at the destination — the spheroid stays lifted through the in-well retract, isn't lifted more than needed, and arrives at the tip for a **small release dispense** (needle keeps the rest; Post-clean clears the accumulation). **Disengagement** = an extra held aspirate applied as the **fast leading portion** of the total. **New:** `SupportClasses/SpheroidSinkCalibrationStore.py` (atomic-JSON singleton + standalone `SinkCurve` with monotone/clamped `lift_for_time`/`time_for_lift`; needle-independent — volume↔lift uses the *current* bore area); `SpheroidPickupConfig` fields (`disengage_enabled/volume_uL/rate_uL_s`, `sink_timing_enabled`, `travel_margin_s`, `release_enabled/volume_uL`) + `to_dict`; `PickPlaceExecutor` gains `bore_area_mm2`/`sink_curve` + `_estimate_travel_time_s` (XY dist ÷ measured/limit XY speed + Z retract/lower + overhead; div-0 safe) + `_planned_aspirate_uL`; `_execute_spheroid_pickup` rewritten. **GUI** (`spheroid_pickup_workflow.py`): "Sink timing & disengage (optional)" settings section + `_current_config`/`_on_start` wiring & gates (needle Ø + calibrated curve required when sink timing on) + `_bore_area_mm2()` + calibration-status label + `hideEvent` hook. **New dialog** `gui/pages/workflows/spheroid_sink_calibration.py::SinkDisengageCalibrationDialog` (modeless; live microscope; worker/bridge; staircase + disengage-test panels; every worker/Abort/hide ends at Safe Z; ZP-connected/Safe-Z/plate-bottom/bore-area preflight gates). **All new behavior OFF by default → byte-identical legacy sequence.** Aspirate=`−`/dispense=`+`, spheroid moves keep `relieve=False`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_disengage_and_sink_timing.py` (21); spheroid-pick-place-z / workflow-settings-popout / pump-settle / cell-targeting / cell-labeling / picker-scaling suites green (142+). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_ENVELOPE_RESTORE_FROM_SETUP.md` | v7.5.x **fix — on restart a calibrated pump's LOCATION restores but its soft-limit MIN/MAX do not** (operator). On ME3B V3, P2 came up `[0, 30]` instead of the calibrated `[-30, 0]`. **Root cause:** the envelope had two persisted sources that drifted — `device_profile.pump_setup` (captured raw extremes, authoritative: P2 `raw_dispensed=0, raw_aspirated=-30` ⇒ `[-30,0]`) vs the separate `safety_limits.p2_min/max` mirror (stale, sign-flipped `[0,30]`). At startup `apply_pump_convention` restored the **datum + direction sign** from `pump_setup` but **not the envelope** (deferred to "the safety_limits section separately"), and `set_hardware_config`'s `update_from_hardware_config(skip_pumps=[…])` clobber-guard then **preserved** the stale mirror (it only blocks the syringe-stroke estimate; it never re-derives the calibrated envelope) → calibration owned datum+direction but not the envelope it captured. Secondary re-clobber: `app.py::_apply_and_save_after_restore` mirrors persisted `safety_limits` back into the live envelope on restore-prompt accept. **Fix:** `StageController.apply_pump_convention` now **re-derives the soft-limit envelope from the captured extremes** (`lo,hi = min/max(rd,ra)`, exact extremes/no margin, matching `apply_pump_setup`) so datum+direction+envelope all come from the one authoritative `pump_setup` on every boot (self-heals future drift); plus corrected the stale on-disk `safety_limits.p2` `[0,30]`→`[-30,0]` in `settings.json` + `config/hardware/devices/ME3B V3.json` so every source agrees (closes the `_apply_and_save_after_restore` hole). Uncalibrated pumps (no `pump_setup`) unaffected — still recomputed from the syringe stroke each boot. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V3.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.py` (25, +`test_envelope_restored_overrides_stale_mirror` + envelope assertion in `test_restore_sign_and_datum`); pump-plunger/common-axis-speed/zp-envelope-absolute/jog-pump-fill suites green (62). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_WELL_AUTO_DETECTION.md` | v7.5.x **model-driven well auto-detection on a plate mosaic** (operator, 2026-07-30: *"detect 24 wells on the mosaic and do the best effort to ensure that they are where they should be and they are the size they should be … lets use that for the auto detection of wells button … we may have different parameters for a black bottom glass plate, or other well plate types … then we will make a nice classifier"*). **The button never worked on this plate:** `MosaicWellMappingDialog._auto_detect` called `WellDetector.detect_filled_wells` **and nothing else** — a generic blob finder, while on a clear plastic plate in brightfield the well is **a hole, not a bright blob** (interior and plate body read the same grey; only the moulded rim is bright) → measured **0 detections** on the operator's own mosaic, so the button reported "couldn't find the grid" every time on exactly the plate being scanned (the scan path only fared better because it falls through to Hough at <3; the dialog had no fallback). Neither used the fact that **the answer's shape is already known**: the plate definition gives 4×6 wells 19.3 mm apart 15.6 mm across and the mosaic carries its own px/µm, so the well radius and lattice spacing in PIXELS are known to ~1 % before looking at a pixel. **NEW GUI-free `SupportClasses/PlateWellDetector.py`**, four stages: **(1)** `ring_response` = matched filter with an annulus kernel (−1 just inside the known radius, +1 just outside), each lobe normalised to unit weight so it is a **difference of local means** — invariant to a stitched mosaic's tile-to-tile brightness steps; on the real plate the 24 wells score **22.8–45.0 while the best spurious peak scores 8.8** (2.6× gap), 0.02 s at ¼ res. **(2)** `fit_lattice` fits rows×cols as **rotation + per-axis scale + translation, deliberately NO shear** (a moulded plate cannot shear, and allowing it lets a wrong assignment hide in a skew), rotation seeded from a 90°-wedge angle histogram + a coarse sweep and translation found by **voting** (every (candidate,node) pairing implies one offset; the true one is voted for by every well at once) — which both labels the detections AND **manufactures the missing ones**, so the result always carries exactly rows×cols wells. **(3)** `refine_ring` walks a radial profile along 180 rays, takes the strongest correctly-signed sub-pixel step per ray, drops sectors that disagree (label text, bubbles, a neighbour's rim) and circle-fits the survivors — it MEASURES the well instead of assuming the catalogue size. **(4)** plate-level **regularisation**: a radius that is a MAD outlier or a centre that wandered off the lattice falls back to the plate median / lattice prediction (a moulded plate's wells are identical to well under a percent, so the median of 24 beats any lone disagreeing fit) — the "best effort to ensure they are where they should be" half. **🐞 TWO FINDINGS THAT SHAPED THE CODE: (a) inlier COUNT cannot score the lattice fit** — it saturates at "all wells" for a whole family of wrong fits (a 5° tilt still lands every well inside a 0.25-pitch gate), and a first cut scoring by count settled on a **−4.99° tilt with 6 % anisotropic pitch** and dragged the refinement with it; rotation is now chosen by **minimising the inlier residual**, count only ranks the discrete hypotheses. **(b) a WIDE radius band is actively harmful** — at ±30 % the radial walk on a low-contrast plate latched onto a shoulder outside the well and reported **18.38 mm for a 15.6 mm well**, where ±10 % measures **15.58 mm** on the same image; `diameter_tol` 0.30→**0.10** and `ring_band_frac` 0.10→**0.06**, both picked by a sweep over the three real mosaics. **Refusing beats guessing** (placing a full grid of wrong markers is worse than "I couldn't find it"; the 3-corner manual teach stays armed on every refusal): **lattice-inlier fraction ≥ 0.60** catches a wrong plate type — a 96-well grid on the 24-well mosaic still "measures" **79 %** of its nodes (the walk finds *some* edge inside those big wells) but only **11 %** are real rings — and 0.60 not 0.50 because a square grid also admits a **45° lattice at √2× the pitch** that lands on exactly half the wells; **measured fraction ≥ 0.25** catches a wrong appearance profile (forcing the wrong polarity still snaps half the lattice but measures nothing). A **180°-flipped lattice fits equally well** (the plate is symmetric — nothing in the IMAGE says which corner is A1) and the two solutions put row/col labels on opposite corners, so the fit is **canonicalised to the near-0° solution**; which corner is really A1 stays the caller's decision from the plate-orientation convention, not from pixels. **PER-PLATE-TYPE PARAMETERS = the path to the classifier:** everything appearance-dependent lives in `WellAppearance`, carried on `PlateType.well_detection` (conditional-serialised → every existing plate type round-trips byte-identically); a black-bottom glass plate needs **a different edge polarity, not different code**. `edge_polarity="auto"` (the default) runs both polarities and keeps whichever locks the lattice better — so a brand-new product works untuned — and **reports the winner** so it can be stored; presets `clear_plastic_rim`/`bright_disc`/`dark_disc`. Those stored profiles ARE the labelled training set a classifier would need, produced as a by-product of ordinary use. `nest-plastic-24.json` stamped `clear_plastic_rim` now the operator's scan settled it (deterministic, 0.38 s vs 0.51 s). **⭐ RESULT on the operator's mosaic (3000×1944, 1410 tiles), 0.4 s: 24/24 wells, ALL measured (none filled from the grid), Ø 15.63 mm vs 15.60 nominal (+0.2 %), well-to-well radius σ = 0.95 px = 38 µm, rotation −0.30°, centre-vs-lattice RMS 3.5 px = 140 µm** — every ring sits on the inner edge of the bright moulded rim. **A free cross-check falls out:** the plate is a manufactured ruler, so a systematic pitch error is evidence about the **mosaic's µm/px** — measured 19.14 mm vs 19.30 nominal says this mosaic's scale is ~**0.9 % small**, and it warns (the older `24` mosaic measures 19.32/19.40 — that one's scale is right). Other mosaics: `24` 23/24 + 1 from the grid (Ø 15.22, RMS 3.3, incl. wells clipped at the mosaic edge); `plate-24_Rossette A1` (low-contrast fluorescence) 23/24, **Ø 15.59** — the case the ±30 % band got wrong at 18.38 mm, the one that motivated the tighter default. Robustness: 4 wells blacked out → 20 measured + 4 from the grid, rest ≤ 8.8 px; illumination gradient 0.55→1.45× → ≤ 0.2 px; noise σ25 → ≤ 0.9 px; rotated 3°/12° → 24/24; ±5 % scale error degrades gracefully + warns. Also **brightness = max over channels, not `BGR2GRAY`** (a fluorescence mosaic can be near-pure blue, which the luma weights all but discard — std 47.8 → 5.6 on the real blue mosaic). **Deliberately NOT done:** the legacy fallback is REMOVED from this button (keeping it would let a wrong grid be placed silently, defeating the gates, and it returns nothing on this plate anyway); the mosaic-scan path keeps its own old detection (not broken, and the finished scan now auto-opens this dialog) — a clean follow-up. **One TEST had to be corrected, not the code:** `test_auto_detect_on_open_places_all_wells` built a synthetic image with 40 px wells 120 px apart while telling the dialog it was a 24-well plate at a scale making those wells 390 px across and 965 px apart — the old detector never read the plate definition so the contradiction didn't matter; a model-driven detector correctly refuses it. **Needs GUI verification on the rig** (Map wells → 24 rings on the wells with the measured Ø in the status line → confirm all → a couple of named wells drive to the right place). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_well_detection.py` (**36** — synthetic plate with a 1.00–1.19 R rim matching the measured real profile, plus a real-mosaic class that skips when the scans aren't on disk); regression plate-types/well-type-presets/mosaic-orientation-remap+adjust/memory-perf/unified-cal/rosette-reanchor/single-well-reregister/startup-well-map/fluor-shift/click-rim/workflow-toggle/fluorescence/v731-integration/last-known-cal/plate-centering/freeform-warp **440 green**. One pre-existing failure confirmed NOT from this change by running the **committed HEAD** copy of `detect_filled_wells` against `24.png` (returns 23, test wants ≥24): `test_v75x_plate_mosaic::test_real_24_well_mosaic`, the legacy blob detector this work doesn't touch — the new detector resolves all 24 on that same image. |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_UNREACHABLE_TRAVEL_STALL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — "the full plate mosaic is not able to get past a single row of images"** (operator, 2026-07-30; *"I think there is an issue with the data flow and management"*). Root-caused from the operator's own `logs/app.log`: the whole-plate scan region IS the configured XY safety envelope (`_ploc_whole_plate_scan_bounds → env`; `ME3B V3.json` records `xy_max_x = 116327 µm`) but the stage physically stopped at **X = 80593 µm** and never moved in X again — while **Y kept tracking its commands exactly**, which is what proves real travel exhaustion rather than the known stale-`R`-ack read desync (a desynced read mis-reports BOTH axes). From that column on `_MosaicScanWorker` (a) **ignored `wait_for_xy_arrival`'s return value entirely**, (b) burned the **flat 10 s** timeout on every remaining point, (c) grabbed a frame anyway and **stitched a DUPLICATE tile at the same canvas spot** (feeding the overlap registration a bogus measurement), and (d) hit the serpentine reversal, which starts the next row at the *far, unreachable* end — so row 1 re-ground the same dead band. ~2 min of dead waiting per row against ~30 s of real scanning ⇒ the scan never visibly left row 0, and nothing warned that the mosaic would be silently partial. **Fix — judge arrival from the MEASURED position, never the boolean** (`wait_for_xy_arrival` is documented to time out spuriously under the stale-`R` desync, so gating on it would DROP tiles the stage did reach; a garbled `(None,None,None)` read degrades to the legacy place-at-target behaviour), **don't stitch an off-target tile** (`_ARRIVE_TOL_UM`=150 µm — its frame shows somewhere else), and reuse that ONE read for placement (the tile used to read position twice, once in the wait + once after the grab). **Distance-sized arrival timeout** `2·dist/max_xy_speed + 2 s` clamped 3–20 s (a dead 3.1 mm step costs ~3 s, not 10). **Runtime reachable-travel box** (`_note_unreachable`/`_reachable`) clipped only after **3 consecutive** non-arrivals — three, so one comms hiccup can't permanently truncate a 900-tile scan — and only on the axis AND direction that fell short (a stage short in +X keeps its whole Y range); points outside it are then **skipped before any move** while still emitting `progress` so `n/total` reaches the end. Worker exposes `unreachable_skipped`/`reach_note`; `_ploc_on_mosaic_finished` captures them before releasing the worker and warns that the mosaic is partial. **Skip-and-continue, NOT abort** — the reachable area still maps correctly, so aborting would discard good tiles. **The envelope-vs-origin question is deliberately left to the operator:** the taught map spans X 10162→106427 µm with `taught_a1=(105618, 65890)`, i.e. the stage COULD reach ~106 mm when taught, and a Prior sets position 0 at power-on wherever the stage sits — so a saved *absolute* envelope + taught well map only line up across a power cycle if the stage starts from the same place; both causes are named in the message rather than guessed, since silently rewriting `safety_limits` would guess at a value governing every clamped move on the machine. **Also fixed en route:** the **`comtypes` DEBUG firehose** — the Nikon Ti panel polls the COM object model ~1 Hz and `comtypes` logs ~10 DEBUG lines per COM call, ~**35 000 lines / several MB during this one 4-minute scan**, rotating `logs/app.log` every few minutes and burying the app's own output (diagnosing this required filtering it out); `main.py::setup_logging` now pins `comtypes` (+`PIL`/`matplotlib`) to WARNING while the app's own modules stay DEBUG. Deliberately NOT changed: this run's **5 % overlap / 2 ms settle** — the code already warns about both at scan start (25 % / 300 ms recommended) and they're operator knobs. **Needs real-HW verification** (scan advances past row 1 at ~1 s/tile; if travel is still short the dialog names the stopping point; then settle whether `xy_max_x` is too large or the origin moved — drive to a known taught well and see if the needle lands on it). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_unreachable_travel.py` (9 — completes-not-hangs, 12/39 tiles stitched with **15 moves instead of 39**, short-axis-only clipping, single-transient-miss doesn't clip, arrived-tile-stitched-despite-lying-wait, failed-read fallback, timeout scaling/clamp/no-`safety_limits`); regression plate-mosaic worker/UI/bounds/settings/detect-fit/reanchor/overlay/reentrancy + orientation-remap/orientation-adjust/memory-perf/unified-cal/rosette-reanchor/single-well-reregister/startup-well-map/fluor-shift/click-rim/workflow-toggle/fluorescence/nikon-ti/z-retract **309 green** (`TestManualAlignPage` excluded — documented pre-existing hang). |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_MEMORY_AND_OVERLAY_PERF.md` | v7.5.x **fix — mosaic capture crawls past ~300 tiles (RAM 80-90%) + app laggy after build** (`changes_needed.md` items 1 & 2). **Root cause:** `MosaicBuilder._records` retained EVERY raw frame (~2 MB → ~½ GB at 300 tiles) + the float64 `_composite`/`_weight_sum` (~190 MB) lived the whole scan → swap thrash → seconds/tile; and `JogWorkspaceView._paint_mosaic_overlay` re-scaled the ~18 MB mosaic pixmap with a 180° painter transform on EVERY paintEvent. **Fix:** new `MosaicBuilder(retain_frames=False)` frees each tile's frame right after `stitch_incremental` blends it (full-plate scan only — that path never re-blends; per-well `build_mosaic` + the calibration dialog `tile_images_px` keep `retain_frames=True`; `build_mosaic`/`_ensure_composite`/`tile_images_px` skip freed frames defensively); new `free_accumulators()` releases the float64 buffers post-scan keeping the uint8 display cache (called by `_MosaicScanWorker.run` after detection); the overlay now caches the scaled+rotated pixmap keyed by `(w,h,flip,source-id)` (pan/needle-motion reuse it; the manual nudge is size-invariant; invalidated on `set_mosaic_overlay`). | `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_RELIEF_AND_BORE_BEAD_MODEL.md` | v7.5.x **two operator-confirmed changes from the workflow-move audit (`coding plans/Analyses/MEBP_WORKFLOW_MOVE_AUDIT.md`): F-1 one bead model + F-4 redesigned pressure relief.** **F-1 (bead model):** the deposited volume-per-mm is now **inner-bore cross-section × extrusion modifier** everywhere (the "thickness of the laid line"), replacing the legacy **outer-Ø × layer_height** in `FlowPhysics.extrusion_flow_rate` + `GeometryEngine.compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` (all gain a `modifier=1.0` kwarg; `layer_height` kept as a param but **no longer scales volume** — it is now only the Z step between stacked layers). Three independent **toggles, all default ON** (`pump_relief_on_pickup/deposit/quick_move`). `StageController` gains `pump_relief_percent()`/`pump_relief_uL(pump)`/`pump_relief_on_*()`; `move_pump_uL(relieve=True)` is now **direction-aware**: an ASPIRATE (pickup) → DISPENSE the relief back (bleed vacuum, existing); a DISPENSE (deposit) → ASPIRATE the relief back (**suck-back, stop drool**, new), each gated by its toggle. `PrintManager._print_pump_suckback` does the **deposit** suck-back (end of each `_execute_print_path`) and the **quick-move** suck-back (before each inter-object hop `MOVE_XY`; re-primed by the next object's prime) — the quick-move case also addresses `changes_needed.md` item 5. **Scope decision:** deposit/quick-move relief is **ink-printing only** — the volume-balanced spheroid/cell **micro-captures keep `relieve=False`** (a 1 %-of-syringe relief = ~2.5 µL would dwarf their nL volumes and break net-zero). **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Timing" relief spin → **% of syringe** + 3 checkboxes (`_rebuild_config`/`_apply_config_to_ui`); the 4 prep-workflow "Common — Pump (global)" rows + the Common Print Settings page row swapped to `pump_relief_percent`; `CommonPrintSettings.GLOBAL_KEYS`/`DEFAULTS` updated. **F-2 follow-on:** the global 10 s settle was a residual-pressure workaround now superseded by this relief — operator can lower `pump_settle_time_s` (config, no code). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1/V3.** Tests: new `tests/test_v75x_pump_relief_and_bead_model.py` (14: bore-area math, modifier scaling, layer-height-ignored, %→µL per pump, deposit/quick-move suck-back + toggle gating, migration); updated `test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (relief now % + direction-aware) + `test_v75x_common_print_settings.py` (key rename); relief/settings/bead (71) + workflow-settings/4-workflow-pages/sketch (153) + print-path (62) + config-serialize (85) suites green. **multi-object seam** — new `_get_path_segments_from_objects()` (one point-list per object, position-offset applied) + `_build_current_job` passes `path_segments=`/`return_home=True` to `build_well_plate_job` (one object → byte-identical legacy plan; empty → meander fallback `path_segments=None`); consumes stashed `intra_well_hop_z_mm`/`line_move_*_speed_mm_s`/`travel_speed`. **NOTE (honest scope):** the seam fix lands on the **discrete** path (`_build_current_job`, the FALLBACK); the full print's DEFAULT executed path is **hybrid** (`_generate_print`→`PrintTrajectoryPlanner`+`PrintPlanOfAction`), whose in-well multi-object trajectory is separate + NOT changed here — a hybrid/trajectory seam pass is a deferred follow-up (keep `execution.mode`=\"hybrid\"; the trajectory planner has a known ZDIR=+1 gap on ME3B V1). **(3) Shared settings + import:** `set_common_print_settings` plumbed wrapper → `WizardPrintSetupPage` → legacy (reaches it via the existing `_fanout_common_print_settings`→workflows-mode fanout; globals already applied at execution via the controller). New **\"Load from Quick Print profile…\"** button on the Finalize Print-Parameters group → `_on_load_quick_print_profile` (chooser over `WorkflowSettingsStore(\"quick_print\")` profiles / last-used / import-file) → `_apply_quick_print_profile(values)`: maps `speed_pct`→`speed_scale_spin` + `printz`→`print_height_spin` (exact), `extrusion_mod`→`volume_fraction_spin` (×100 clamp 1..100 — documented semantic adapter), `preflow`→per-pump prime µL (= derived flow × s), `line_retract`/`line_*_speed`/`travel_speed`→stash; **SKIPS** ink/prep/postclean and **NEVER** writes back globals (`g_settle`/`g_relief`), well positions, or calibration (decodes combo `{text,data}` tokens). Safety invariants preserved (retract-before-XY via existing primitives; end-at-safe-Z; `z_up_sign`/`plate_axis_sign` stamped; calibrated positions never re-signed). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (Workflows → Full Print → build a multi-well/multi-object plate print → Monitor → runs to taught wells, ends at safe Z; \"Load from Quick Print profile…\" fills the Finalize controls).
