| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_Z_AUTOCAL_TAB.md` | v7.5.x **moved the focus-based per-well Z-bottom auto-cal to its own "Plate Z Auto-Cal" tab, after Plate Location**. The old "Needle Offset Calibration" tab bundled the needle's vertical *reference heights* (Replace/Max/Fast-Move/Plate-Top/Plate-Bottom Z) **and** the per-well Z auto-cal (Step 1 first-spot teach + Step 2 focus auto-cal + live microscope feed). But the auto-cal drives to each calibration well, so it needs the finished **Plate Location** XY map — yet it sat *before* Plate Location. Split: `_build_z_offset_tab` keeps only the reference heights + estimate-from-needle-cam + Go-to-Replace-Z (these must precede Plate Location, which retracts to Fast-Move/Safe Z between wells) and gains a pointer note; new `_build_plate_z_autocal_tab` holds Step 1 + Step 2 + the live feed. New tab order: Needle Location → Needle Offset Calibration → Plate Location → **Plate Z Auto-Cal** → Custom. The moved widgets keep their existing attribute names (`_zauto_*`, `_zoff_btn_run_z`, `_zoff_btn_cancel_z`, `_zoff_lbl_auto_z`, `_zoff_live_view`) so every handler (`_zauto_goto_first_well`, `_start_auto_z_cal`, `_auto_z_set_progress/running` — already `getattr`-guarded) works unchanged; `_zauto_tab_index=3` and `_on_workflow_tab_changed` now starts the live camera on the new tab. Pure UI relocation, no motion/coordinate math. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_z_autocal_tab.py` (8, real offscreen page build). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** |
| `MEBP_v75x_NEEDLE_LOCATION_QUICK_MOVE.md` | v7.5.x **"Go to needle location" quick-move on the Calibration → Needle Location tab**. The side cameras (NEEDLE_X/NEEDLE_Y) are bolted to the frame, so the centered needle position is a stable per-machine datum; the button drives straight there (retract Z → XY → lower to the saved needle-cam Z) so the needle re-enters both side views ready to re-center, instead of hand-jogging it into frame. Saved as `device_profile.needle_loc_xy_um` (absolute Prior stage µm — power-cycle-stable; new `DeviceProfile.needle_loc_xy_um` round-trips it) on each successful Center & Save, plus a manual "Set current as location" button to seed a fresh machine. `_needle_loc_goto` reuses `_safe_navigate_to`→`safe_travel_to` (Z target = `get_needle_cam_z_user()`→`user_z_to_zref`; stays at Safe Z if no cam-Z); gated like the Plate Location run when ZP-connected + Safe Z unset (a retract to zero-ref 0 = bottom datum = crash on ME3B V1). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_needle_location_quick_move.py` (17). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_WELL_XY_OFFSET.md` | v7.5.x **fix — mosaic well↔XY correlation off by ≫ one field of view**. After a full-plate Mosaic scan the stitch + auto well-fit looked great but driving to a fitted well landed several mm off. Root cause: tile pixels are placed at the **trusted raw stage frame** (`MosaicBuilder._canvas_origin_um`; the shift is NEVER applied to pixels), but `_ploc_fit_from_mosaic_detections` back-projected detected wells with `canvas_extent_um[0:2] + px/scale` — and `canvas_extent_um` **adds** `_global_shift_um`. So every well taught to the warp was translated by the shift; the warp absorbs it as a clean translation (fit looks great, residuals tiny) and every commanded XY is off by the shift. `_global_shift_um` is pre-seeded **unbounded** from `MosaicAlignmentStore`; on ME3B V1 it held a stale **(−3170, −5135) µm** (≈2–3 FOV) value written by a `manual_align` overlay nudge later relabeled `quick_fov`. Proven on disk: `well_training/24/labels.json` A1 `um=(5520,4921)` = shifted back-projection; `last_calibration.json` `taught_a1=(5520,4921)` (saved cal carries the offset); true A1 ≈ (8690,10056). Overlay + corrupt saved wells shifted together → looked aligned while XY was wrong. Fix (`gui/pages/calibration.py`): new `_ploc_mosaic_world_shift()` (live builder's `_global_shift_um`, else (0,0)); back-projection now uses the **trusted-stage origin** `extent[0:2] − shift` (exact inverse of placement) so navigation is independent of any overlay/registration/manual shift; seeded `init_shift` **bounded to ±2 FOV** (larger = corrupt, ignored+logged). Overlay/`manual_align` unchanged (display-only). Cleared the corrupt `shift_um` from `mosaic_alignment.json`; saved calibration NOT hand-edited (TPS warp) — **re-run the Mosaic scan** to regenerate correct positions. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_mosaic.py` (114, +2 new). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_MOSAIC_SCAN_AND_OVERLAY.md` | v7.5.x **full-plate mosaic scan → detect-all-wells → toggleable overlay** (fixes per-well auto edge-detect failing on single frames). New **Mosaic scan** button on Plate Location rasters the whole plate (QTimer tick loop modelled on `_start_well_scan`; stays retracted at Safe Z via `_safe_navigate_to(lower_z=False)` — microscope images from overhead), feeds the existing `MosaicBuilder` (stage-coordinate stitch + phase-correlation + feathered blend; `canvas_extent_um`/`_mosaic_scale` transform), then `_ploc_detect_and_fit_from_mosaic` runs new `WellDetector.detect_wells` (multi-circle HoughCircles) on the stitched field, back-projects each circle px→absolute stage µm (`stage=extent[0:2]+px/scale`), matches each predicted well→nearest unused detection within tol, and routes matched pairs through the existing `_ploc_feed_affine`→`_manual_fit_xy` warp. New `SupportClasses/MosaicStore.py` persists the composite PNG (`config/hardware/mosaics/<plate_key>.png`) + absolute-µm extent (`plate_mosaics.json`, atomic, keyed by plate). New `JogWorkspaceView` overlay (`set_mosaic_overlay`/`set_mosaic_visible`/`has_mosaic` + module `pixmap_from_bgr`): extent stored absolute, shifted by `_zero_off` at paint (like the envelope) + rotated 180° under `_FLIP_DISPLAY_180` so it registers with wells/needle; drawn under the wells. **Show mosaic** toggles on Plate Location + Jog (Jog loads via `MosaicStore` on `set_calibration_data`/`load_startup_plate`; Plate Location reloads on build/`set_hardware_config`/`_on_plate_changed`). **Rev 2 (bench feedback):** scan moved off the GUI thread to a background `_MosaicScanWorker(QThread)` (camera grab timer + UI no longer freeze; worker only commands thread-safe moves + samples frames via new `CameraWidget._frame_seq`/`frame_count_value()` — waits N fresh post-move frames, never touches the backend); `capture_fresh_frame(discard_n_frames,settle_ms)` drains the OpenCV FIFO (per-well scan uses 5/120); raster overlap → **25%** (`_PLOC_MOSAIC_OVERLAP`); scan bounds **clipped to the reachable XY envelope** + out-of-envelope points filtered (`_ploc_clip_scan_bounds`; no more edge clamping); **live "Mosaic (live)" preview** pane per tile; detection now on the **incremental** composite (coordinate-faithful to `canvas_extent_um`; `build_mosaic`'s registration branch desyncs origin/scale); worker aborts after 8 consecutive no-frame tiles (reports dropped); re-entrancy guard (busy flag + Run disabled before the modal objective dialog; `_ploc_run_queue` checks `_ploc_mosaic_running`); `_shutdown_mosaic_worker()` wired into `MainWindow.closeEvent`. Decisions: position-based stitch (accurate Prior II encoders), new button not a replacement, persisted to disk. **Hotfixes:** missing `QPixmap` import made the button a silent no-op (Qt swallows slot exceptions) → fixed + handler wrapped so failures always surface; tile-count confirm (a full-plate microscope mosaic is thousands of tiles); **"Settings…" dialog** (`gui/dialogs/mosaic_settings_dialog.py`) to tune camera settle/timing (default 300 ms settle after each move — the "camera couldn't keep up" fix), FOV overlap, mosaic resolution, and HoughCircles detection sensitivity/tolerance, persisted in `settings.json` (`mosaic_scan` section). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_mosaic.py` (36); full `test_v75x*` = 572 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_DISCONNECT_WATCHDOG_DEBOUNCE.md` | v7.5.x **ZP disconnect-during-print, round 2** — after round 1 (`MEBP_v75x_ZP_DISCONNECT_DURING_PRINT.md`) suspended the poller during `PRINT_PATH`, the ZP still dropped via the **independent port-health watchdog**. Log proof: the 16:11 run's final `retract_to_safe_z` returned in **0.011 s** (= `ensure_retracted_to` early-out on `is_zp_connected False`) — ZP was already gone before the print ended **while the poller was suspended**, so the trigger was `SerialUtils.ConnectionWatchdog`, which fired `_handle_disconnect("ZP")` on the **first** `check_port_health` failure (`serial.in_waiting` raises intermittently under a print's heavy write load on Windows — a transient, not a real drop). The 16:12 run's `MOVE_Z` took exactly **0.5 s** (the fixed-settle fallback = `is_zp_connected False`) and the print **silently dry-ran to a bogus `completed`**. (Periodic M500 ruled out — gated on `_zp_auto_save_position`, default `False`.) Fixes: **(A)** `ConnectionWatchdog` now **debounces** — `watch(..., fail_threshold=3)` + per-watch `fail_count`; a disconnect needs `fail_threshold` **consecutive** failed checks (a healthy check resets the window), so a single transient `in_waiting` failure is ridden out while a genuine unplug (fails every check) is still caught; `_check_loop` body extracted to `_run_one_cycle()` for testability. **(B)** `PrintManager._execute_loop` records `_zp_connected_at_start` and, on a mid-print ZP drop, **ABORTS** (state `ERROR`) + logs a `zp_disconnected` exec-log event instead of dry-running; `_execute_print_path` also halts the path immediately on a mid-path drop (both gated on `_zp_connected_at_start`, so an XY-only/no-ZP job is unaffected). This is the round-1-deferred #4 backstop, now warranted. Disconnects are now visible in the print JSONL (exact step) + the console distinguishes port-health vs poller-liveness. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_disconnect_watchdog_and_abort.py` (7); full `test_v75x*` = 551 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_LOCATION_Z_SIDE_VIEW.md` | v7.5.x **Z side view on the Calibration → Plate Location tab** so the operator can move the needle **up to safe travel manually** while teaching wells. Reuses the existing `XZSideView` (the Jog page's side view): built as `_ploc_xz_view` in `_build_plate_location_tab` and placed in a horizontal `QSplitter` **beside `_ploc_plate_view`** (top of the left column, above the live microscope feed). Its green **"Safe"** badge fires `go_to_z_requested(z_mm)` (raw zero-ref) → new `_on_ploc_go_to_z` → `move_z_absolute(z, from_zero_ref=True)` (soft limits respected; no-op when ZP disconnected — mirrors `jog_control._on_go_to_z_requested`; pure up-Z retract = the manual-jog exemption). `on_status_update` feeds live needle Z (logical, zero-ref) + the X/Z zero offsets; `_refresh_ploc_view` keeps the envelope / needle / `z_up_sign` display-sign / Z references in sync (so the Safe badge updates the moment Fast Move Z is set, since `_set_safe_z → _emit_calibration_data_changed → _refresh_ploc_view`). No new motion/coordinate math. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_z_side_view.py` (6); regression `test_v731_jog_navigation` + `test_v75x_plate_centering`/`z_retract_before_xy_travel`/`last_known_calibration` (74) + `test_v75x_plate_location_manual_click_rim` (27) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_SETUP_ROUTINE.md` | v7.5.x **Quick Print "print setup routine" — confirmed descent + 0.25 s pump pre-flow** (after the pre-position+confirm dialog). The defined sequence: (1) Z→safe, (2) XY→print start, (3) Z→print Z (plate bottom + offset) **and confirm the needle arrived**, (4) start pump flow + wait 0.25 s, (5) begin trajectory. The discrete `build_well_plate_job` plan already emitted `TRAVEL_UP→MOVE_XY→MOVE_Z→[prime]→PRINT_PATH` (shape correct); two gaps fixed: **Step 3** — the discrete `MOVE_Z` handler now CONFIRMS Z arrival (`flush_moves` M400 + `wait_for_z_arrival`, **poller suspended** for the wait, try/finally, graceful fallback to the old fixed settle when ZP-disconnected/older-controller/mock; non-arrival logs `move_z_timeout`, no abort) instead of a blind `sleep(0.5)` — generalized to all discrete prints. **Step 4** — `QuickPrintWorkflowPage._build_settings` sets `prime_amounts_uL[pump] = flow × _PREFLOW_S (0.25)` at `pump_rates_uL_s[pump] = flow`, so `build_well_plate_job` emits the `EXTRUDE` prime at the flow rate (≈0.25 s of pump flow) immediately before `PRINT_PATH` (scoped to Quick Print; Print Setup configures its own prime). Builds on `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_POSITION_CONFIRM_AND_NO_HOME.md`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_setup_routine.py` (6); full `test_v75x*` = 522 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_DISCONNECT_DURING_PRINT.md` | v7.5.x **fix — ZP board "keeps getting disconnected" mid-print** (reported via Quick Print; mechanism general). Root-caused (6-reader workflow + `logs/prints/*.jsonl`): cached ZP z reproducibly goes `None` ~4–8 s into the `PRINT_PATH` loop yet every job logs `completed` — a **false-positive** `_handle_disconnect("ZP")` while the board is alive. The `PositionPoller` is left **active** during `PRINT_PATH` and its `M114` reads contend with the dense per-segment pump-write stream; `get_current_position()` didn't drain the RX buffer before `M114` and `receive_data()` did one 10 ms non-blocking `read_all()`, so the reply isn't there → `_last_position_read_ok=False` → 8 consecutive fails (`max(5,int(2.5/0.3))`≈2.4 s) → `on_zp_lost`. Compounded by `resume()` never resetting `_zp_fail_count`. Fixes: **(#1)** discrete `PRINT_PATH` wraps `_execute_print_path` in new guarded `StageController.suspend_position_poller()`/`resume_position_poller()` (try/finally — poller cache is display-only there); **(#2)** `PositionPoller.resume()` resets `_zp_fail_count` (fresh window); **(#3)** `ZPStageManager.get_current_position()` `reset_input_buffer()` before `M114` + bounded ≤0.25 s early-exit wait for a complete reply (busy board ≠ dead board; lock released between reads; true power-off still reports the failure → v7.5.0 reconnect-hotfix preserved). Trajectory/velocity/hybrid are NOT suspended (their `PrintRecorder` reads the poller cache) — protected by #3+#2. **#4 abort-on-genuine-disconnect deferred** (must follow bench-confirmation of #1–#3, else false positives become spurious aborts). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_disconnect_during_print.py` (11); full `test_v75x*` = 510 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ALL_PRINTS_END_AT_SAFE_Z.md` | v7.5.x **CRITICAL SAFETY — guarantee every print leaves the needle at a safe Z on ANY outcome (completion / error / abort), in ALL modes (discrete / hybrid / trajectory)**. Previously each path relied on the *plan* ending with a retract step (`TRAVEL_UP` / `RETURN_HOME` / final waypoint) and **none retracted on the error/abort exits** — a print that errored or was aborted mid-pattern left the needle parked down in the well (the trajectory planner's `ZDIR=+1` Z-geometry gap made its final retract unreliable on ME3B V1 too). Fix = new `PrintManager._retract_to_safe_z(context, travel_z=None)` — polarity-safe, **raise-only**, best-effort (never raises) via `StageController.ensure_retracted_to` (confirmed no-op when already retracted; `move_z_absolute` fallback on older controllers) — called in the discrete `_execute_loop` `finally` (covers completion + error + the abort early-returns) and in `gui/app.py`'s `_hybrid_thread` / `_traj_thread` `finally` blocks (passing `job.settings.travel_z_height` explicitly, since hybrid doesn't set `pm.job`). State is set before the `finally` so the GUI isn't delayed; `abort()`'s existing prompt GUI-thread retract is left as-is (the `finally` is the guarantee; `PRINT_PATH` checks the abort flag mid-path so the loop reaches `finally` quickly). Complements `MEBP_v75x_Z_RETRACT_BEFORE_XY_TRAVEL.md` (retract *before* travel) + `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_POSITION_CONFIRM_AND_NO_HOME.md` (Quick Print pre-position). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_always_safe_z.py` (9); full `test_v75x*` = 470 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_POSITION_CONFIRM_AND_NO_HOME.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Quick Print** changes. **(1) Pre-print position confirmation** — before any extrusion the stage now travels (needle **retracted** to the safe travel Z, never lowered) to the print-start point (well center + first path point of the first object) so the operator can verify the needle location on the live microscope, then a confirm-position dialog gates the descent+print. New `QuickPrintWorkflowPage._preposition_for_print(start_zref_mm, travel_z)` → `StageController.safe_travel_to(x_um, y_um, safe_z_mm=travel_z, target_z_mm=None)` (raise→wait→XY→wait, **never** lowers); `_on_print` restructured to build settings up-front → pre-position (buttons disabled + `self._status.repaint()` during the blocking move, same GUI-thread pattern as `CalibrationPage._safe_navigate_to`) → confirm dialog (inline ⚠ if the move reported a timeout; the move's `False` return does not auto-abort) → run. **(2) CRITICAL — no return-to-origin** — `build_well_plate_job` gains `return_home: bool = True`; when `False` the trailing `HOME_XY` ("Return home" = `move_xy_absolute(0,0)`) is omitted while the final `TRAVEL_UP` retract is kept, so the print ends by lifting the needle out of the well and **stopping where it finished** instead of driving the plate back to (0,0). Quick Print passes `return_home=False`; default `True` keeps Print Setup / `PrintPlanOfAction` plans byte-identical. Complements `MEBP_v75x_Z_RETRACT_BEFORE_XY_TRAVEL.md`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_position_confirm.py` (9). |
| `MEBP_v75x_JOG_DIRECTION_Z_UP_SIGN.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Z jog was reversed + Xbox "clamped and kept moving constantly"**. Two Z-direction sign systems had diverged: the per-machine `z_up_sign` (=+1, DERIVED by the Set Bottom/Top setup, loaded at startup) vs the hardcoded module `ZDIR=−1` and the hardcoded jog-button signs — because `steps_per_mm.Z` was flipped to **−5255** (M92 sends the sign to Marlin, inverting the raw Z frame), so the setup correctly re-derived `z_up_sign=+1` but the jog inputs still encoded the old `+5255` polarity. Result: on-screen **Z▲ drove the needle DOWN**, keyboard **PageUp drove UP** (opposite each other), Xbox stick reversed, while the readout (z_up_sign) disagreed with all of them. Fix (user-confirmed *z_up_sign authoritative*, all jog inputs): jog signals now carry a **HEIGHT-frame** delta (+=up); new `StageController.move_z_user_relative(user_dz)` maps it to raw via `Δraw=Δuser·z_up_sign` and is the single entry point for every jog input. `move_z_relative` **drops the dead `_flip_sign("Z")`** (Z axis-flip UI was removed in v7.4.2, force-zeroed — Z direction is now z_up_sign-owned; pumps keep `_flip_sign`). `JogButtonArray` Z▲=+1/Z▼=−1 (contract = height-frame); all 5 consumers (`control_panel._on_jog_z`, `stage_panel._on_jog_array_z`, `calibration._zteach_jog_z`, `settings_page._ctx_jog_z`, `jog_control._key_jog_z`) route through `move_z_user_relative`. Xbox `ZPJogHandler` gains a `z_up_sign_provider`; loop dz = `vz·seg·z_up_sign` (algebraically identical to the old `−vz·seg` at `z_up_sign=−1`, correctly flips at +1). **Clamp runaway** fixed by new module fn `_shorten_only_delta(cur,requested,clamped_dest)` — a relative jog's soft-limit clamp may only SHORTEN the move (never reverse sign or amplify magnitude), so an out-of-bounds *cached* position can't synthesize a large opposite-direction delta re-sent every segment (the constant-motion bug); applied in `move_z_relative`/`move_pump_relative`/ZP jog loop `_clamp_delta`. Module `ZDIR=−1` left as a latent non-jog fallback inconsistency (noted). **Verified on ME3B V1 bench (2026-06-16):** Z▲/PageUp/stick-up retract toward the taught Top, numbers increase, soft-limit jog no longer runs away. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_direction_z_up_sign.py` (20). |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_TRAJECTORY_VIEW.md` | v7.5.x **Quick Print gains a live trajectory + needle ghost-trail + camera view** (display-only; print path unchanged). New `gui/widgets/print_trajectory_monitor.py::PrintTrajectoryMonitorView` — an auto-fit top-down canvas (zoomed to the selected well, not the full plate) drawing the **planned** waypoint path (per-object blue polylines + dots, dashed-grey inter-object travel), a persistent **executed** polyline (green), and a **fading mauve ghost trail** at the needle (breadcrumb algorithm copied from `JogWorkspaceView` — `deque(maxlen=24)`, >1 µm gate, `alpha=20+120·t`), plus an optional well-boundary circle; all in **zero-ref µm** with the same 180° display flip so overlays register. `quick_print_workflow.py`: new `camera_manager` ctor param; `_build_main_area()` lays out a vertical `QSplitter` (top horizontal `[trajectory | CameraFeedView]`, bottom `WellPlateNavigator`) with resizable boundaries everywhere; microscope feed bound via `camera_for_role(MICROSCOPE)`, started on `showEvent`/stopped on `hideEvent` (only if we started it, gated on `is_running`); `_refresh_planned_path()` (well/object/size change + `set_calibration_data`/`set_hardware_config`/`showEvent`) builds segments from the existing `_path_segments_for_selection()` offset by `_well_center_zero_ref_mm`×1000; `_push_live_position()` in `on_status_update` feeds `get_xy_position(cached=True)−zero_position` (the 300 ms tick — `PrintManager.on_progress` is per-command, no live XY); `reset_live()`+`set_recording(True)` at print start, `set_recording(False)` on terminal state. `workflows_mode.py` passes the shared `camera_manager` to the page. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_trajectory_view.py` (13); quick-print + sketch/seam suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_LIFT_AND_THICKNESS.md` | v7.5.x two Print Builder → Sketch additions. **(1) Lift between shapes** — surfaced the existing `Sketch.travel_clearance_mm` (compiler already lifts inter-path travel to `z_travel = z_start + num_layers·layer_height + travel_clearance_mm`; it was stuck at the 2.0 default with no UI) as a new "Lift between shapes" spin (0–40 mm) in the print card, wired via `_set_sketch`. **(2) Show line thickness** — optional toolbar toggle (`_thickness_btn` "line" icon → `_toggle_thickness` → new `SketchCanvas.set_show_thickness`); `_draw_toolpath` strokes **print** runs at the deposited bead width `max(1px, line_spacing_mm·scale)` (≈ needle Ø; α140, round cap/join) instead of the thin 1.6px line, so multi-pass/fill toolpaths visually show how thick the printed lines will be (travel runs stay thin/dashed; toggle defaults off). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_lift_and_thickness.py` (6); sketch+quick-print suites = 56 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_WELL_BOUNDARY.md` | v7.5.x **Sketch page becomes well-aware: default single-well zoom + selectable well + needle-safe boundary**. The Print Builder → Sketch canvas already authors shapes in well-relative mm with (0,0)=well center and drew one dashed "reference well" ring (`SketchCanvas.set_reference_well`, sized from the *max* well Ø) framed by `fit_view()`. Added: (1) **per-well selector** — new persistent "Well boundary" card (`_well_combo` populated from `WellPlate.well_names`, default **A1** else first) so the boundary derives from a *chosen* well's `WellInfo.diameter` (fallbacks: uniform `well_diameter` → max well Ø); `set_hardware_config` now `WellPlate.load(config.active_plate_key)` + `_apply_well_boundary()`. (2) **inner needle-safe ring** — `SketchCanvas.set_safe_boundary(Ø)` + `_draw_safe_boundary()` (red dashed, "needle-safe Ø" label) at inner Ø = `well_Ø − needle_Ø` (inset by the needle *radius* per side, from `NeedleSpec.od_mm`); outer ring relabeled "well wall Ø". (3) **default zoomed single-well view** — new `SketchPage.showEvent` re-fits when the sketch is empty (keeps pan/zoom mid-sketch); `_apply_well_boundary` also `fit_view`s on well change. (4) **non-blocking out-of-bounds guard** (user choice = warn/allow): `_recompute_preview` flags any waypoint with `sqrt(x²+y²) > safe_radius` (mirrors `print_objects._check_bounds`, all waypoints incl. in-well travel) → warning label; `_send_to_print_setup` confirms before sending. Removed superseded `_well_diameter_from_config`. No new transform/coordinate math. Designed via a 4-agent understanding workflow. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_well_boundary.py` (13); all sketch suites = 34 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_CAMERA_HARDWARE_CONTROLS.md` | v7.5.x **camera-side (firmware/SDK) controls + real resolution change + authoritative readout** — distinct from the software `CAMERA_IMAGE_CORRECTION` post-process. Motivated by the **BUC3D-1000C**, which enumerates via the **ToupTek SDK** as `C3CMOS10000KPA` (probed live: gamma 20–180/def100, brightness ±64, contrast ±100, exposure 200–2e6 µs, gain %, auto-exposure ON, eSizes 3664×2748/1832×1374/912×686). Three gaps fixed: hardware controls weren't exposed (backend had only `set_auto_exposure`/`set_resolution_index`); the Microscope **Resolution** combo only set the µm/px *spec* — never `put_eSize` (never reconfigured the camera); no readback existed. **`toupcam_backend.py`**: `get/put_Brightness|Contrast|Gamma|ExpoTime|ExpoAGain` + `get_AutoExpoEnable` via lazy signature-safe `_bind`/`_get_scalar`/`_put_scalar`/`_get_triple` ctypes helpers; `get_exposure_time_range`/`get_exposure_gain_range`; `get_eSize`; `get_resolution_list` (queries the **open handle** — works around EnumV2 returning an empty res list on this SDK build); `get_settings()` device readback; `HW_RANGES`. **`camera_widget.py`**: cross-backend `hardware_capabilities()`, source-labelled `get_hw_settings()`, `set_hw_auto_exposure/exposure_us/exposure_gain/gamma/brightness/contrast`, `set_capture_resolution()` (ToupCam→nearest eSize+pull-mode restart; OpenCV→`CAP_PROP_FRAME_*`/`CAP_PROP_*`), `log_hw_settings()`. **`camera_manager.py`** delegates per slot. **`camera_feed_view.py`**: ⚙ gear overlay top-right, shown only when `hardware_capabilities().controllable` (ToupCam/OpenCV; not sim/stopped), opens a reused modeless dialog; new `enable_settings` kwarg (False on the dialog's own preview → no recursion). **NEW `gui/dialogs/camera_settings_dialog.py`**: modeless pop-out — caps-gated resolution combo / auto-exposure toggle (manual exposure+gain disabled while auto on) / exposure-ms / gain / gamma / brightness / contrast sliders, live-apply + per-identity persist, "⟳ Read from camera" + "Defaults", monospace readout mirror, small live preview. **`CameraCalibrationStore.py`**: `get/set_hw_controls` (nested `hw_controls`, None-dropped; µm/px + image_correction siblings preserved). **`hardware_setup.py`**: `camera_started → _on_camera_started_hw` restores persisted controls + **always logs the read-back block** (`[camera start] … source = toupcam`); `_maybe_apply_resolution_to_device` makes the spec Resolution combo also drive the live device. **Readout-cannot-lie invariant**: every shown/logged value comes from SDK getters against the open handle + always carries a `source` field. Changing capture resolution logs a "µm/px may need redoing" warning; `enable_settings` defaults True. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_hardware_controls.py` (17; `FakeToupCam` injected into a real `CameraWidget`). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CAMERA_IMAGE_CORRECTION.md` | v7.5.x **per-camera gamma / brightness / contrast correction**. `CameraWidget` already had software brightness (beta) + gamma (LUT) applied in `_grab_frame` *after* the raw frame is cached for detection, but **no contrast** and no exposed UI (the manager builds widgets `show_controls=False`) and no persistence. Fix: (1) `CameraWidget` gains `_contrast`/`set_contrast`/`contrast` + a contrast slider; brightness+contrast now apply in one `convertScaleAbs(alpha=contrast, beta=128−128·contrast+brightness)` so contrast pivots on **mid-gray** (no incidental brightening; identical to the old brightness-only path at contrast=1.0); + `image_correction()` snapshot / `reset_image_correction()`; setters resync their own sliders. (2) `CameraManager` per-camera `get/set_brightness|contrast|gamma`, `image_correction`, `set_image_correction` (partial), `reset_image_correction` — **delegate to the persistent `CameraWidget`** (single source of truth; survives stop/start/source-change). (3) `CameraCalibrationStore` `get/set_image_correction` stores `cameras[identity]["image_correction"]={brightness,contrast,gamma}` — identity-keyed/per-machine, sibling to µm/px+rotation (preserved). (4) Hardware Setup → Cameras: a per-slot **Image Correction** strip (3 sliders + Reset) beneath each live preview, pushes live to the manager + persists on `sliderReleased` by device identity; `_restore_calibration_for_slot` re-applies stored correction (independent of µm/px) + syncs sliders. **Display-only** — the corrected frame flows to every `CameraFeedView` via `frame_captured`, but the raw `_current_frame` for detection/calibration stays untouched. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_image_correction.py` (18, incl. real frame-math + raw-frame-uncorrected invariant). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_PRINT_VISIBILITY.md` | v7.5.x **fix — a freshly-sketched print didn't appear in Quick Print (until restart) and rendered a blank preview**. Two independent regressions from the v7.5.0 Print Builder. **(A) Quick Print list stale:** `QuickPrintWorkflowPage._refresh_objects()` re-scans `config/prints/` correctly but was only called at construction + the manual refresh button; `print_file_created` (`app.py::_on_print_created`) is wired only to the Print Setup tab, never the workflows page → stale combo until a restart rebuilt the page. Fix = new `showEvent` → `_refresh_objects()` (preserves selection via `findData`; catches prints from any source on every entry). **(B) CSV pointer key mismatch:** `PrintFileManager.save_trajectory_as_print_object` wrote the CSV pointer as `csv_path` but every `csv_import` reader looked for `source_file` → `import_csv_trajectory` never fired → empty trajectory → blank preview/path (manual CSV import worked: it stores `source_file`). Fix = writer now emits `source_file` (+ `csv_path` alias for back-compat); the 3 readers (`print_objects._build_print_object`, `print_setup_legacy`, `quick_print_workflow._obj_dict_to_path_points`) accept `source_file` **or** `csv_path` (so already-saved sketch files preview without re-save). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_print_visibility.py` (9). |
| `MEBP_v75x_Z_AXIS_CONVENTION_RETHINK.md` | v7.5.x **unified Z convention — 0 at the needle-down datum, + going up, always positive, established by ONE setup**. Root cause: Z lived in 5 frames and the display conversion was applied 3 inconsistent ways (control panel `−raw` ignoring zero; jog `−(raw−zero)`; calibration captures `raw−zero` with no sign), and the recorded refs were incoherent (`plate_bottom_z` read *higher* than the travel height). Fix = one canonical frame `user_Z = z_up_sign·(raw − zero["Z"])` with the datum at the **mechanical hard-bottom** and `z_up_sign` **derived** by the setup. `StageController`: `_z_up_sign` (default module `ZDIR`) + `raw_to_user_z`/`user_z_to_raw`/`zref_to_user_z`/`user_z_to_zref`/`z_up_sign()` (all `getattr`-fallback safe) + `apply_z_setup(raw_bottom, raw_top)` (sets datum + derives sign + sets the raw soft-limit envelope + `direction_ok`); `z_height_of`/`default_travel_z`/`print_z_dir` generalized to `z_up_sign()`. **Needle-cam → plate-ref guesses** (per user): `_needle_loc_center_and_save` captures the needle-tip-camera Z fiducial (edge clicks = tip bottom corners), and `estimate_plate_z_refs()` pre-fills Plate Top/Bottom/Fast-Move Z from 3 editable **standard offsets** (mm below the fiducial) on the Device page; Max/Replace stay manual. Persistence via `DeviceProfile`/`settings.json` (`z_up_sign`, `needle_cam_z`, `plate_z_offsets`) + `apply_z_convention()` restored at startup (`main.py`). New **Z Axis Setup** group on Hardware Setup → Device (Set Bottom=0 / Set Top + offsets). GUI sweep routes every Z readout/spinbox/limit/capture through controller converters (stage_panel, control_panel, standard_jog_context, jog_control, calibration). Migration = re-run the setup. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_z_axis_unified_setup.py` (12); updated `z_display_numbering` + `cal_z_envelope_no_clobber`; 213 Z/cal/jog tests green. **Needs real-HW verification** (bench checklist in the plan). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_LOCATION_Z_FIRST_AND_RETRACT.md` | v7.5.x **safety: Z-heights-first ordering + retract the needle before every Plate Location inter-well hop**. The run retracted only **once** pre-scan; each per-well move (`_ploc_begin_well_click_rim` anchor, `_ploc_advance` freeform, `_ploc_auto_fit_well` initial, `_ploc_begin_well_manual` start) was a bare `move_xy_absolute_um` at whatever Z — so after the operator jogs Z down to focus on a well's edge (manual click-rim), the hop to the next well dragged the lowered needle across the plate; the pre-scan fallback also used `safe_z=0.0` when unset (a descent on ME3B V1 ZDIR=-1). Fixes (all `gui/pages/calibration.py`): (1) **reordered workflow tabs** to Needle Location → **Needle Offset Calibration (Z heights)** → **Plate Location** → Custom (`_zoff_tab_index` 2→1; only consumer was `_on_workflow_tab_changed`); (2) **run gate** — `_ploc_run_queue` blocks when `controller.is_zp_connected` and `_safe_z is None`, directing to the Needle Offset tab to capture Safe/Move Z first (gated on ZP-connected so an XY-only rig isn't blocked); (3) new **`_ploc_safe_goto(x,y)`** retracts to Safe Z + travels XY via `_safe_navigate_to(lower_z=False)` (raise-Z→wait→fast-XY→wait, never descends; bare fallback only if Safe Z unset), wired into all inter-well moves. **Intra-well rim-sample moves (`_ploc_multi_edge_fit`) stay bare** (orbit the rim at imaging Z; retract/refocus each step would be wrong+slow). Complements `MEBP_v75x_Z_RETRACT_BEFORE_XY_TRAVEL.md` (extends its guarantee to every per-well hop). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_manual_click_rim.py::TestSafeGoto` (2) + stub bind; offscreen tab-order check; Z-retract(22)/autocal/camera suites green |
| `MEBP_v75x_CALIBRATION_REFERENCE_MARKERS.md` | v7.5.x **permanent registration markers for taught well centres on the plate view AND the live microscope overlay** (during calibration + after) so the operator can confirm the map/image register to the physically-taught points. New `CalibrationPage._reference_markers` (well → absolute stage µm = the circle-fit centre) populated live as each well is taught (`_ploc_finalize_click_rim`, 3-point `_ploc_confirm`) + in bulk from auto-fit at `_ploc_finish_run`; `_reference_in_zero_ref()` converts for the plate view. `JogWorkspaceView.set_reference_markers(dict)` draws a distinct **pink ⊕** (ring+cross+label, zero-ref µm) above the wells in the clipped layer; `CameraFeedView.set_reference_markers(markers, stage_x_um, stage_y_um, um_per_px)` + `set_reference_stage_position()` project each marker into the live frame (`(x−stage)/µm_per_px + w/2`, camera centre = stage centre) as a pink ring+cross, tracking stage motion (base-class overlay; `TargetOverlayCameraView` unaffected). `_refresh_ploc_view` pushes to both (plate zero-ref; camera abs µm + microscope-slot µm/px + cached stage centre) and is called on each well taught; the position loop pushes the live stage centre. Persisted in `_save_calibration` (`reference_markers:{name:[x,y]}`) / restored in `_load_calibration`; cleared on a real plate-format change. Color pink (unused; wells green/yellow/blue, needle red, toggle mauve). No new transform math. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_manual_click_rim.py` (`TestReferenceMarkers` + finalize assertions; 25 in file) + offscreen render smoke; affected suite (85) green |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_LOCATION_POST_CAL_FIXES.md` | v7.5.x three fixes surfacing right after a manual click-rim plate calibration. **(A) Free/Snap toggle obscured** — `JogWorkspaceView.paintEvent` painted the mode toggle *first* then plate/wells on top, so a well near the top-left covered the buttons; now clips workspace drawing to `_content_rect()` and paints the toggle **last** (hit-testing unchanged). **(B) Stage drove to an envelope corner** — a degenerate 3-well manual fit (close/near-collinear/noisy clicks) gives an affine whose linear part is wildly stretched; `correct_positions` extrapolates distant wells off-plate, and navigating to one **clamps to the XY envelope corner** (`safe_travel_to`/`move_xy_absolute*` already clamp, so not truly out-of-range but a wrong extreme). Fix rejects the fit: new `PlateWarpCalibrator.linear_scale_range()` (min/max singular value of the linear part) + `CalibrationPage._warp_is_plausible(warp)` rejects when either axis scale ∉ [0.8,1.25]× or |rotation|>30°; `_manual_fit_xy` gates on it after `solve()`, keeping prior positions + prompting re-teach. Bounds the **linear part only** — translation is limited by reachable travel and the plate may exceed the travel (ME3B V1 envelope-vs-footprint), so an absolute/containment test would wrongly reject every fit. **(C) Custom-tab plate diagram shrank to a speck** — `_update_cal_view_plate` fed `_cal_plate_view.set_taught_a1` the page A1 in absolute **µm** while that QGraphicsView positions dots as `well_abs_mm − a1_mm` (expects **mm**); the 1000× error ballooned `itemsBoundingRect` → `fitInView` shrank the plate; fixed by `/1000`. Navigation already clamps (no true out-of-range motion); fix removes the *cause*. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_manual_click_rim.py::TestFitSanityCheck` (4) + offscreen render/build smoke; affected suite (81) green |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_Z_REFERENCE_VECTOR.md` | v7.5.x **print-Z up-direction derived from the plate-bottom→plate-top reference vector** instead of the hard-coded `StageController.ZDIR=-1`. On ME3B V1 the needle descends as raw Z increases, so several print-offset sites that assumed "larger Z = higher" inverted: `build_well_plate_job` stacked layers `+layer*layer_height` (drove **deeper** each layer — the limitation flagged in `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_Z_PLATE_BOTTOM_DATUM.md`), `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._well_print_z` did `top_z + ink_z_mm` ("Larger Z = higher"), and the plate-bottom datum/floor used the constant `ZDIR`. New module fn `derive_z_up_sign(plate_top_zref, plate_bottom_zref, fallback=None)` = `sign(top−bottom)` (plate top is physically above bottom) with a **live**-`ZDIR` fallback (None-sentinel, so monkeypatched `SC.ZDIR` works); new `StageController` plate-top datum (`set/get_plate_top_z`, pushed from `app.py::_update_print_floor_datum` alongside plate-bottom) + `print_z_dir()` (uses `getattr(...,None)` for `__new__` test ctrls). `print_height_to_zref`/`zref_to_print_height`/`print_floor_violation`/`_apply_print_floor_raw` now pass `self.print_z_dir()` to the existing `zdir`-aware helpers. Direction carried into the discrete path via new `PrintSettings.z_up_sign` (default `+1` = legacy additive), stamped from `controller.print_z_dir()` by the GUI builders (`print_setup_legacy._get_settings` via a mock-safe `_print_z_dir()` that `float()`-coerces + falls back to `ZDIR`; `quick_print_workflow`); `build_well_plate_job` + `_well_print_z` step offsets by `z_up_sign`. **User-scoped to print offsets only** — Jog/Device display numbering, soft-limit swap, and the planner's broader travel/safe-Z gates stay on `ZDIR`. Canonical input stays "height above plate bottom (+=up)"; `ink_z_mm` stays "rel. plate top" but now sign-correct (negative = into well) on both polarities. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_z_reference_vector.py` (16); full `test_v75x*` = 305 green |
| `MEBP_v75x_Z_AUTOCAL_MANUAL_SEED.md` | v7.5.x **Z-bottom auto-cal seeds from a manually-calibrated first spot + live microscope feed on the Needle Offset tab**. User-confirmed design: (1) the operator drives to the first calibration well and jogs Z to the bottom while watching a new passive live `CameraFeedView` (`_zoff_live_view`, bound to the Microscope slot, started on `_workflow_tabs.currentChanged` via `_on_workflow_tab_changed` + `_zoff_ensure_live_camera`, rebound in `_refresh_ploc_view`), then **Record first-spot Z** (`_zauto_record_first_spot` → `_zauto_seed_z` + `_auto_z_results[first]` + `_z_teach_points[first]`); (2) auto-cal runs only the **remaining** wells seeded from that taught Z (no separate "approximate Z" field — the manual first spot *is* the seed). Root cause fixed: the legacy sweep computed `estimated_bottom = top_z - well_depth` and **stepped Z down (subtract)** to descend — hardcoded for ZDIR=+1, so on ME3B V1 (ZDIR=-1, needle descends as zero-ref Z *increases*) it walked the needle *away* from the plate. Reworked `_auto_z_tick` to a **polarity-general** height-above-reference parametrization: new `_auto_z_move_to_h(h)` (h = mm above the reference bottom, + = safe/away; `plate_relative_to_zref(ref, h)` → zero-ref Z), "toward plate" = decreasing h, floor = `h <= floor_h` (seeded: `[-tilt_margin, approach_margin]` = [-0.5, 1.0] mm around the seed; legacy: `[0, 2.0]` above the estimate). `_start_auto_z_cal` pre-seeds the first well + starts at `index(first)+1`; dual-widget `_auto_z_set_progress`/`_auto_z_set_running` sync the Custom-tab + Needle Offset tab. Scoped to `gui/pages/calibration.py` (+1 import). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_z_autocal_manual_seed.py` (13) |
| `MEBP_v75x_Z_RETRACT_BEFORE_XY_TRAVEL.md` | v7.5.x **CRITICAL SAFETY — guarantee Z retracts to the "move"/safe Z before every cross-position XY move** (except within-well print/workflow moves + manual jog). Motivated by two reported failures: (1) Jog-page well click drove straight to the well without retracting; (2) Quick Print returned to (0,0) at the end without retracting. Root cause #1: the click-to-travel gate `if current_z >= self._safe_z - 0.05: <direct XY move>` is **polarity-wrong** on ME3B V1 (`StageController.ZDIR=-1`, needle descends as raw Z increases) — it skipped the retract exactly when the needle was DOWN; duplicated in `jog_control._on_workspace_position_clicked` + `spheroid_pickup_workflow._on_workspace_position_clicked`. Root cause #2: discrete `MOVE_XY`/`HOME_XY` relied on a separate preceding `TRAVEL_UP` (no self-retract, no Z-arrival wait) and Quick Print's `travel_z_height` fell back to a raw `5.0` (a *descent* on ZDIR=-1). Audited **all 68 XY-move call sites** via a parallel workflow (classified cross-position / within-well / manual-jog / calibration-scan / bypass). Fixes — new polarity-safe `StageController` helpers `z_height_of`/`needle_at_or_above`/`default_travel_z` + `ensure_retracted_to` (raise-to-height-and-wait, **never descends**; also fixed `safe_travel_to`'s insert-floor comparison to the height frame); 3 GUI click gates now ALWAYS `safe_travel_to` when Safe Z known; `PrintManager._retract_for_travel` invoked at the top of `MOVE_XY`/`HOME_XY`; HybridPlanExecutor `TRAVEL_XY` now blocking-`move_z` before XY; Quick Print fallback → `default_travel_z`; calibration `_jog_xy_home`/`_goto_a1`/`_goto_corner`/`_goto_well`-fallback routed through `_safe_navigate_to`; one-time pre-scan retract in `_ploc_run_queue`; `PickAndPlaceManager._intra_well_move` XY µm/mm scaling bug fixed. **Known gap:** `PrintTrajectoryPlanner` Z geometry still assumes ZDIR=+1 (logs a warning; prefer discrete/hybrid-direct on ME3B). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_z_retract_before_xy_travel.py` (22). |
| `MEBP_v75x_LAST_KNOWN_CALIBRATION_RESTORE.md` | v7.5.x **"load last known calibration" so a restart with an unchanged setup doesn't force a full recal**. Persists a durable last-known-good snapshot bundling **needle zero** (`zero_position`) + **plate** (taught wells/warp/affine + format) + **Z** (z-plane + Safe/Top/Max/Plate-Bottom/Replace heights) + a **hardware fingerprint** (device profile, axis_map, steps/mm, plate format, needle gauge) + timestamp to `config/hardware/last_calibration.json` (new `SupportClasses/CalibrationSnapshotStore.py`, atomic os.replace, modelled on `CameraCalibrationStore`). **Root cause of the loss:** `calibration.py::_on_plate_changed` cleared all plate cal with no same-format guard, so a programmatic/duplicate plate-combo change wiped a just-loaded calibration and the 500 ms debounced auto-save persisted the nulls (matched on-disk state: `taught_a1`/`z_plane` null, Z heights intact). Fixes: (1) same-format early-return guard in `_on_plate_changed`; (2) `CalibrationPage._has_plate_calibration()` keystone gate (taught A1 / warp / non-identity 3-well) — `_save_calibration` writes the snapshot only when true so an empty page never clobbers a good snapshot; (3) `app.py::_maybe_prompt_calibration_restore` (mirrors `_maybe_prompt_zp_position_restore`) fires once/session via `QTimer.singleShot`, **only when live plate cal is empty** (no nag on healthy restart), applies needle zero (software ref, no motion) + reloads plate/Z via `_load_calibration`, warns via `fingerprint_diff` when the setup changed; (4) `save_settings` shutdown snapshot flush. Scope = needle+plate+Z only (camera/objective µm/px keep their own identity-keyed stores). **Addendum (2026-06-16):** accepting either restore popup (calibration *or* ZP position) only mutated in-memory state, so the operator still had to manually *Apply + Save* on the Device page (and the restored values were lost until clean shutdown). New `MainWindow._apply_and_save_after_restore()` called from both accept paths mirrors the persisted `safety_limits` into the **live** envelope in place (never rebinding the object the jog handlers hold) and calls `save_settings()` immediately. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_last_known_calibration.py` (20) |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_LOCATION_MANUAL_CLICK_RIM.md` | v7.5.x Plate Location **manual (jog-driven) click-rim well fit + live microscope view + objective confirmation**. New operator-driven well centering on the Calibration → Plate Location tab: a **Well-fit mode** combo (`_ploc_mode_combo`, default **Manual**, alt **Auto**=legacy edge-detect) + a live `CameraFeedView` (`_ploc_live_view`) under the plate map (vertical splitter). In Manual mode the stage drives **once** to the predicted well center (anchor); the operator then **jogs** (Xbox/Jog page) to bring each rim edge into the live view and **clicks the well edge** — each click records the rim point at the stage's **current** position (so jog adjustments are honored) and never moves the stage. Clicks convert to absolute stage µm via the existing pipeline (`CameraFeedView.clicked` → `CameraManager.pixel_to_stage_offset` center-offset×µm/px → `+ get_xy_position(cached=False)`, the absolute-µm frame shared with `_predict_well_xy`/`move_xy_absolute_um`, `xy_position_scale=1`); ≥3 clicks → circle-fit (`fit_circle_to_points` + `_PLOC_RADIUS_TOL`) → center → `_ploc_well_results` (feeds the affine/warp like auto + 3-point). New `well_click_rim` pause kind: `_ploc_begin_well_click_rim` (anchor) / `_ploc_on_live_view_click` (record at live pos, no move) / `_ploc_update_click_rim_prompt` / `_ploc_finalize_click_rim`; **Confirm**=fit collected (≥3), **Skip**=skip the whole well. **(Revised from a first cut that drove to 8 predicted rim points + auto-advanced after each click — the teleport fought manual jogging and the predicted points, being what's calibrated, often showed no edge; `_PLOC_RIM_SAMPLES`=8 is now just the suggested click count.)** Pre-run **objective gate** `_ploc_confirm_objective` shows `camera_config.current_objective_name` + in-use µm/px (+ stored-cal note) and requires Yes (blocks if no objective selected); applies to both modes. Live cam started in Manual (`_ploc_ensure_live_camera`) + rebound to the Microscope slot in `_refresh_ploc_view`. No new transform/motion math. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_manual_click_rim.py` (17) + offscreen page-build smoke |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_LOCATION_PREDICT_WELL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Plate Location "Snap to well center" drives to the gap between wells**. The run state machine moves to each snapped well via `CalibrationPage._predict_well_xy(well)` → `move_xy_absolute_um` (absolute µm). Two defects in that one method: (1) its primary branch read `getattr(self, '_predicted_wells', None)` — an attribute assigned **nowhere** (grep-verified) — so it never fired; (2) the geometry fallback then returned `zero_position + (well−A1) grid` (well grid pinned to stage zero). But the canvas draws from `_wells_in_zero_ref` → `_calibrated_positions or _predicted_positions` (absolute µm, centered on the **XY-envelope midpoint**), minus `zero_position` for display. So **display and motion used different frames** → a constant offset that still looks grid-aligned but lands between wells (~9 mm pitch). The dead branch also meant a fitted warp never fed back into later runs. Fix: `_predict_well_xy` now reads `_calibrated_positions or _predicted_positions` (same precedence as the canvas) and returns that well's value **directly** (absolute µm, no `zero_position` offset — the old `zero+value` was for the zero-ref dict that never existed); fallbacks clarified (taught-A1 anchor, **new** envelope-center seed mirroring the canvas, then last-resort zero anchor). No caller/motion-math change; both callers (`_ploc_auto_fit_well`, `_ploc_begin_well_manual`) already feed absolute µm. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_predict_well.py` (10) |
| `MEBP_v75x_MULTI_OBJECT_PRINT_SEAM.md` | v7.5.x **fix — multi-object Quick Print extruded across object seams** ("toward the end of each spiral it jumps to a new location, drives through the printed material, then continues"). Root cause proven from the log `print_20260615_…PIAR…C4.jsonl` (audit GEO-3/FRM-7b): `PIAR.json` = 6 spirals on a **hexagonal ring** (2 mm centres, 2 mm radius, ~309 pts each, centre→rim), concatenated into ONE `PRINT_PATH`; the 1.9–4.3 mm rim→centre seams ran as **extruded** moves at print Z. **Geometry, NOT timing/queue** — recomputing the gap from the logged XY targets reproduces the jumps exactly (the commanded path itself jumps); they land on the 5 object boundaries (309/618/927/1236/1545); overlapping spirals make a seam *look* "mid-print". (Separate real finding: that pre-fix run's `drift_s`→27 s / `lag_um`~2.35 mm median = open-loop pacing+SMS, audit MOT-1/2/3 — smooth lag, not the jumps.) Fix: `build_well_plate_job` gains optional `path_segments`; per-well loops `segments=[s for s in (path_segments or [path_points]) if s]`, **first object** = full `TRAVEL_UP→MOVE_XY→MOVE_Z` approach, **later objects** = small **1 mm hop** (`MOVE_XY{hop_z}`, `hop_z=print_z+z_up_sign·intra_well_hop_z_mm`, new default `PrintSettings.intra_well_hop_z_mm=1.0`) routed through `_retract_for_travel(target_z=hop_z)`→`ensure_retracted_to` (polarity-safe raise-only, blocking, insert-floored) so it clears thin print without a ~21 mm full retract; only `PRINT_PATH` extrudes (seam = pure travel). `path_segments=None` (print_setup_legacy / PrintPlanOfAction) → byte-identical legacy plan. `quick_print_workflow`: `_path_segments_for_selection()` (one sub-path/object), `_path_points_for_selection()` flattens it, `_on_print` passes `path_segments=`, already stamps `settings.z_up_sign`. PIAR rebuild: 6→6 PRINT_PATHs, worst in-PRINT_PATH seg 4.288→0.197 mm, obj 1 full approach + obj 2–6 1 mm hops (ME3B z_up=-1: print_z −16.17→hop_z −17.17). Residual 0.197 mm = constant-angle `generate_spiral` (GEO-2, separate). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_multi_object_print_seam.py` (11) |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_EXECUTION_LOGGING.md` | v7.5.x **print execution logging** (debug feature for the Quick-Print "circle stopped → drove to 0,0" and "spiral jumpy at end" HW bugs): new `SupportClasses/PrintExecutionLogger.py` writes one JSONL per print run to `logs/prints/` (schema in `logs/prints/README.md`) for **all** modes — discrete (Quick Print), hybrid plan, trajectory playback. Captures: `job_start` manifest (full settings + command plan + `zero_position` + safety limits), per-command boundaries, per-PRINT_PATH-segment events (target/clamp/sleep/pump-vol incl. `vol_dropped`/`drift_s`), `settle_wait` ok/timeout (previously a silent debug-level continue), trajectory waypoint lateness, blocking-move arrival results, abort/error+traceback, and a 5 Hz background sampler of **cached** poller positions with `lag_um` vs last commanded target (zero extra serial traffic). Hooks: `PrintManager` (auto-create on `start()`, close in `_execute_loop` finally), `TrajectoryExecutor`/`DirectCommandExecutor`/`HybridPlanExecutor` (+`exec_logger` param), `gui/app.py` hybrid+trajectory launch paths, Quick Print status shows the filename. Kill switch `MEBP_PRINT_LOG=0`. Companion audit root causes (BUG-1: trailing HOME_XY preempts the open-loop PRINT_PATH; BUG-2: `generate_spiral` constant-angle sampling → ×17 segment growth at the rim) recorded in the plan's Issues & Decisions. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_execution_logging.py` (13) |
| `MEBP_v75x_XBOX_INPUT_PIPELINE.md` | v7.5.x **Xbox input pipeline overhaul — fixes "everything resets every ~3 s while jogging" + "trigger neutral isn't 0 / axis moves with no input"**. Root causes (multi-agent audit + empirical HW probe): the worker's 3 s heartbeat did `pygame.joystick.quit()/init()`, wiping SDL per-device axis state; an idle pad never re-reports, so triggers read 0.0 **indefinitely** and the Windows −1.0 rest offset manufactured a phantom 0.5 press (the v7.3.4 0.25 s suppression window could never cover it). Worker (`XboxController.py`): heartbeat now non-destructive (JOYDEVICEREMOVED hot-plug events + `get_count()`; `_find_controller`'s quit/init retry kept for connect only), **trigger arming** (axis trusted only after its first JOYAXISMOTION per joystick object), **zero-on-loss** dispatch before reconnecting, same-command groups dispatch one **combined** value (RT−LT; key "4+5"), inactive keys get a final zero (`last_sent` = key→(value,cmd)), dpad-as-buttons NameError (misindented debounce) fixed, "monitor" messages publish raw axis state, `ctrl_queue` applies deadzones/offsets/debug **live**, `stop_event` for clean shutdown, mapping hot-reload keeps last-good on parse failure + editor saves atomically (`os.replace`). Consumer (`StageController.py`): poller consumes monitor msgs into `last_axis` (old `int("0-1")` cache never worked → stick pucks dead, Calibrate Sticks snapshotted zeros), `on_lost` re-zeros jog velocities (reinstates lost v7.3.4 behavior), ZP/XY jog **0.5 s staleness watchdogs** (velocity cmds are Xbox-only — verified), `update_xbox_tuning()`, `_calibrate_zero` log reads → cached (head-of-line blocking on the Processor thread). GUI: `TriggerBar` takes pull [0,1] (was (v+1)/2 → rest rendered 50%); `main.py` headless deadzone parity. **Needs real-HW verification.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_xbox_input_pipeline.py` (19, real worker loop vs fake pygame via `sys.modules`) |
| `MEBP_v75x_Z_DISPLAY_NUMBERING.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Z read −60 at the top and the Min/Max froze Z**. ME3B V1's needle *descends as raw Marlin Z increases* (`steps_per_mm["Z"]=+5255`), but the app numbered Z by the raw value, so "up" went negative and recording the top into Max gave `z_max=-60` / `z_min=0` (inverted → `clamp_z` collapses to 0 → frozen). v7.4.2 had tied direction solely to the `steps_per_mm` sign, so inverting the motor also reversed the hard-wired jog buttons. Fix (user chose: flip *numbering* only, fixed for this machine, whole Jog+Device scope): new `StageController.ZDIR=-1` + `z_raw_to_display`/`z_display_to_raw` — **user-facing Z = ZDIR×raw** applied at the human boundary only (display ×ZDIR, typed/clicked input ÷ZDIR). Motor/motion/print/calibration frames unchanged (no re-cal). Limit spinboxes are height-frame and convert to the raw-stored `SafetyLimits.z_min/z_max` with a **min/max SWAP** (guarantees z_min<z_max → can't re-freeze). Touches: `control_panel` Z readout+bar range; `stage_panel` Z readout + record/load/apply×2/reset (swap); `XZSideView.set_z_display_sign` (display chokepoints; `go_to_z_requested` emit stays raw zero-ref; default +1 = unchanged so spheroid's view is untouched); `jog_control` wires the sign + signs reference labels; `standard_jog_context` converts the Absolute Go-To Z input. Dormant `_axis_flip`/`_flip_sign` stays unused. Existing inverted envelope in `ME3B V1.json` + `settings.json` corrected to `z_min:-60/z_max:0`. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_z_display_numbering.py` (13) |
| `MEBP_v75x_CAL_Z_ENVELOPE_NO_CLOBBER.md` | v7.5.x **fix — every Z jog clamped to a single point (~48.4 mm)**. Triggers mapped to `move_z_at_velocity` logged `Z clamped: 48.280 → 48.400` *and* `48.509 → 48.400` (both directions → same value = collapsed/inverted envelope). Root cause: the calibration page pushed its captured `Max Z` (16.4) → `z_max` and `Plate Bottom Z` (48.4) → `z_min` into the live `SafetyLimits` (`_zoff_set_max_z`/`_zoff_set_plate_bottom_z` + a v7.4.4 `_load_calibration` startup restore). On this machine the needle **descends as Z increases**, so "Max Z" (max retraction) is numerically *below* "Plate Bottom Z" → `z_min 48.4 > z_max 16.4` → `clamp_z` collapses to 48.4 for every input. It also silently clobbered the device-setup Z range (10..60 in settings) on every startup. Fix (user chose Device-tab authoritative): the Z soft-limit envelope is owned solely by Hardware Setup → Device; calibration's Max Z / Plate Bottom Z are kept only as print/Z references (`_max_z`/`_plate_bottom_z`) and no longer call `set_z_from_current`. In-session recovery: re-Apply device settings or restart. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_cal_z_envelope_no_clobber.py` (4) |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_ENVELOPE_ABSOLUTE.md` | v7.5.x ZP (Z + pump) safety envelope → **absolute Marlin raw frame** (twin of `MEBP_v75x_XY_ENVELOPE_ABSOLUTE.md`). Bug: after calibration, Jog-page + Xbox ZP jogs were wrongly clamped (Hardware Setup jog escaped via `bypass_safety=True`). The Z/pump envelope was zero-referenced, so when `reset_z_zero`/Set Z Zero re-anchored `zero_position`, the stored limits (e.g. ME3B V1 `z[10,60]`) no longer matched the working range (needle near zero-ref 0 → excluded → every jog clamped). Clamp code was correct (zero-ref vs zero-ref); the data went stale. Fix: clamp the **absolute** raw destination in `move_z_relative`/`move_pump_relative`/`move_z_absolute` (now also clamps the `from_zero_ref=False` raw path) + `ZPJogHandler._clamp_delta` (Xbox); `_record_limit` + `settings_page` setters store absolute raw; `XZSideView` gains `set_zero_offset_z` + `_z_safety_bounds()` chokepoint (absolute→zero-ref for the Z scale/scrollbar), pushed from `jog_control`/spheroid. Caveat: absolute ZP envelope relies on the raw frame being established (ZP position-restore on connect, or fresh calibration) since Marlin loses position on power-cycle. Migration: reinterpret existing limits as absolute, re-record once. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_envelope_absolute.py` (7) |
| `MEBP_v75x_FREEFORM_INTERPOLATING_WARP.md` | v7.5.x freeform-point plate calibration now **warps the plate to match the control points exactly** instead of a single global similarity fit. New `SupportClasses/PlateWarpCalibrator.py` (affine base + thin-plate-spline residual, pure numpy) with graceful degradation: 0→identity, 1→translation, 2→similarity, 3→affine, **≥4→affine+TPS (exact at every control point, smooth between)**; raw per-point deltas + mean/max correction; raw-pairs `to_dict`/`from_dict` (re-solved on load). `calibration.py::_manual_fit_xy` fits the warp (was `MosaicCalibrator`), stores `_plate_warp`, mirrors the linear part into the legacy `AffineCalibration` via `similarity_approx()` for the scale/rotation label + back-compat `mosaic_affine`. `_ploc_confirm` records per-point `dx`/`dy`; `_ploc_refresh_queue_view` renders `Δ(dx,dy) µm \|mag\|` for taught freeform points; `_ploc_finish_run` shows overall RMS Δ. Persistence: `_save_calibration` writes `plate_warp`; `_load_calibration` restores it **preferentially** (legacy `mosaic_affine` recompute guarded behind `_plate_warp is None`). Scoped to the freeform/manual-teach path only — the 3-well SVD auto-cal (`_accept_plate_scan`) stays on the similarity affine and clears `_plate_warp` so the two paths don't clobber each other. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_freeform_warp.py` (12) |
| `MEBP_v75x_CAMERA_CAL_PERSIST_STORE.md` | v7.5.x fix **camera calibrations lost on next start**: needle/plate µm/px (+rotation) was stored in `HardwareConfig.camera_calibrations` — *inside the swappable setup* — so loading a saved hardware-setup file (no camera cal) replaced the in-memory config and the auto-save wiped it to `{}` (confirmed in settings.json). A camera's µm/px is a property of the *physical camera on its USB port*, so it moved to a **per-machine store**: new `SupportClasses/CameraCalibrationStore.py` (`config/hardware/camera_calibrations.json`, keyed by device identity, holds `{um_per_px,rotation_deg,name,date}` + a `role→identity` assignments map). `hardware_setup.set_calibrated_um_per_px`→store; `_restore_calibration_for_slot`←store. **Auto-restore**: sources aren't persistent handles, so `_remember_assignment` records role→identity and `_auto_assign_sources_from_store` (after Detect) re-selects each slot's source by matching identity → fires source-changed → restores µm/px+rotation (new inverse helper `camera_identity.source_for_identity`; tuple combo match via `itemData`, not `findData`). Removed the vestigial `HardwareConfig.camera_calibrations` field + serialization (legacy key ignored). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_calibration_store.py` (10, incl. survives-file-load + session-2 auto-restore integration) |
| `MEBP_v75x_MICROSCOPE_ROLE_AND_OBJECTIVE_PIXELCAL.md` | v7.5.x two camera cleanups: (1) **Removed `CameraRole.PLATE`** — the microscope camera *is* the camera that views the plate, so MICROSCOPE serves both. Dropped from the enum + `SINGLETON_CAMERA_ROLES`; `HardwareConfig.from_dict` migrates a legacy `"plate"` role string → `MICROSCOPE` (else it'd fall back to UNASSIGNED). Removed the "Plate (overhead)" role-combo item + the `_role_badge_props` PLATE branch in `hardware_setup`; the plate edge-fit auto-cal in `calibration.py` now resolves `CameraRole.MICROSCOPE` (wording "Plate role/camera"→"Microscope"). (2) **Objective calibration now uses the stage-motion `PixelCalibrationDialog`** (same as the needle cameras: move the stage a known distance, phase-correlate the plate-image displacement) instead of the slide-marking `ObjectiveCalibrationDialog`. `ObjectiveCalibrationCard` gains a `controller_getter`, preflight-checks (microscope cam running + stage connected), launches the stage-motion dialog, and persists the measured µm/px **per-objective** via `ObjectiveCalibrationStore.set_calibration` (extended with optional `rotation_deg`); on objective swap it pushes µm/px **and** rotation to `CameraManager` so the live-target picker maps clicks. The slide-marking dialog/`MeasurementCameraView` remain (latter still used by `live_target_picker`) but the card no longer wires `ObjectiveCalibrationDialog`. Tests: updated `test_v744_calibration_revision.py` (+legacy-plate migration) and `test_v74x_objective_calibration.py` (PLATE→MICROSCOPE; +rotation round-trip) |
| `MEBP_v75x_XY_ENVELOPE_ABSOLUTE.md` | v7.5.x two needle-zero (Needle Location) calibration fixes: (1) **crash** — `calibration.py::_needle_loc_center_and_save` called `stage_to_um(value)` missing the required `xy_position_scale` arg; now passes `self._xy_position_scale` (matches `settings_page`). (2) **XY clamp rejected valid moves after re-zeroing** — the XY safety envelope was stored *zero-referenced* (`Record Min/Max` saved `xy - zero`), so after needle *Set Zero* re-anchored `zero_position` mid-travel the zero-ref position sat on the envelope minimum and any negative delta (`dy=-9.2`) clamped to 0 even though the absolute destination was valid. Reworked the XY envelope to the **absolute Prior stage frame** (fixed mechanical extents, independent of Set Zero): `move_xy_absolute`/`move_xy_absolute_um`/`move_xy_relative`/`move_xy_relative_um` clamp the *absolute* destination; `default_plate_center_um` returns `xy_center()` (absolute midpoint) directly; `stage_panel._record_limit` stores the absolute reading. Display widgets fed zero-ref positions (`JogWorkspaceView._envelope_bounds`, `XZSideView._x_bounds_um`) subtract the current zero (new `set_zero_offset`/`set_zero_offset_x`, pushed from `jog_control`/`calibration`/`spheroid` update loops) so the envelope draws coherently and identically in the common case. `control_panel` position bars become coherent (value was already absolute). No auto-migration (record-time zero unknown) — existing envelopes reinterpreted as absolute (correct for ME3B V1's `0..114332`), re-record with one click otherwise. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_xy_envelope_absolute.py` (16) + updated `test_v75x_plate_centering.py` |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_WORKFLOW.md` | v7.5.x new **Quick Print** workflow (enables the stubbed `quick_print` tile): pick one object → click one well → Print. | `MEBP_v75x_CAL_LIVEVIEW_AND_CAMERA_ROTATION.md` | v7.5.x stage-motion **Calculate µm/px** dialog (`PixelCalibrationDialog`) gains: (1) **live view + detection arrow** — two-column layout with a live `CameraFeedView`; after each measure it overlays the phase-correlation displacement as a green arrow (new `CameraFeedView.set_overlay_vector`) so the operator sees what was detected. (2) **Selectable move direction + 45° rotation** — needle cameras are 90° apart but the pair is at ~45° to X/Y, so a wrong-direction move just drives the needle in/out of focus (optical-axis motion, no lateral shift). Dialog adds a free move-direction angle + X/Y/±45° presets; tiny/low-confidence motion is flagged as optical-axis → "try another direction"; the accepted direction (= the camera's lateral stage axis) is exposed as `result_rotation_deg`, stored per camera in `camera_calibrations[id]["rotation_deg"]` + on `CameraManager.get/set_rotation_deg`, and fed to a **generalized `TwoCameraNeedleAligner`** (new `angle_x_view_deg`/`angle_y_view_deg` → 2×2 solve `U·n=s`; legacy `None`→90°/0° reduces exactly to the orthogonal mapping). µm/px scale itself is rotation-invariant; apply scope = needle centering only (live-view click path unchanged). (3) **Cross-contamination fix** — calibrating one needle camera appeared to change the other's: `calibration.py::_ctx_update_um_px_display` pushed a µm/px derived from the *shared* microscope `camera_spec` into the manager for **every** slot on each config change (one fires per calibration via `_on_config_changed`), clobbering the just-set needle calibration; the push is now gated to the `MICROSCOPE` slot only. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_rotation.py` |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_JOG_CLAMP_FREEZE.md` | v7.5.x **fix — ZP board froze & disconnected when jogging into a soft limit**. `ZPJogHandler._jog_loop` (8 Hz) clamps each segment vs. the cached poller position; pinned at a limit the clamp leaves a tiny µm **residual**. `ZPStage.move_relative`'s zero-move gate was `abs(d) > 1e-6`, so the residual passed and the loop emitted **one micro-move every segment forever** (stream of un-acked `G0` → board freeze → poller-liveness disconnect); raw `f"{d}"` also rendered them in **scientific notation** (`0.00009 → "Z9e-05"`) that not all parsers accept. Fix at the emission chokepoint: gate raised to `abs(d) >= 1e-4` mm (0.1 µm — fully-clamped jog now sends nothing), fixed-decimal `:.4f` format (never sci-notation, matches print path), and feedrate floored `max(fr,1)` (an `F0` stalls Marlin's planner — sibling freeze). `move_absolute` also `:.4f`. **Needs real-HW verification.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_jog_clamp_freeze.py` (8) |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_DTR_NO_RESET.md` | v7.5.x **safety fix — ZP axes ran to their extents on app close**. Shutdown issues *no* motion command (verified: `closeEvent → controller.shutdown() → disconnect_zp() → ZPStage.stop() → serial.close()`, only `M500` elsewhere); the motion is the Arduino/Marlin **DTR (and CH340 RTS) auto-reset** — the OS dropping DTR/RTS on `close()` pulses the MCU RESET pin → steppers de-energize (gravity Z drops, backdrivable pumps drift) and/or firmware auto-homes. **First attempt pinned the lines low at open too (kill reset on both ends) — REVERTED: broke connection, the ME3B V1 board needs the open-time DTR reset to start talking.** Current fix is close-time-only: `ZPStage.stop()` de-asserts `dtr`/`rts` **before** `serial.close()` (runs only at shutdown → cannot affect connectivity). Open path left at default `serial.Serial(port,…)`. Effect is board/driver-polarity dependent; reliable cure is **hardware** auto-reset disable (cut `RST-EN` / 10 µF RESET→GND), which keeps serial data so the board still connects. Partially addresses the Layer-6 item deferred in `MEBP_v750_ZP_RECONNECT_HOTFIX.md`. **Needs real-HW verification.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_dtr_no_reset.py` (7) |
| `MEBP_v75x_XY_ZERO_PERSISTENCE_HOTFIX.md` | v7.5.x **XY position wrong on restart** fix (ProScan box stays powered → keeps its absolute frame, but the readout came up wrong). Everywhere XY shows `displayed = raw − zero_position`; `zero_position` is restored on startup (`main.py:207`) but was **only persisted by the explicit "Set Zero" buttons**. Non-button zeroing — the Xbox **"zero_needle_pos"** (`xbox_mapping_editor.py:307` → `StageController._calibrate_zero`) and auto-cal — mutated it in RAM only, and clean-shutdown `MainWindow.save_settings()` didn't save it, so on restart the reference reverted (often to 0) and the retained-correct raw position decoded wrong. Fix: `save_settings()` now also `set_section("zero_position", …)` (guarded) as a clean-shutdown catch-all → restored reference reproduces the pre-close readout. Complements `MEBP_v75x_ZP_POSITION_OVERRIDE.md` (ZP loses firmware position → manual re-declare; XY kept hardware position, only lost software reference). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_xy_zero_persistence.py` (3) |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_POSITION_OVERRIDE.md` | v7.5.x manual **ZP axis position override** (power-cycle recovery): Marlin (ZP) has no absolute encoder, so after a board power cycle it powers up reporting 0 — the position readout is wrong and *Refresh* just re-reads the wrong value (`zero_position` *is* persisted/restored, but the lost value is the firmware counter, which only the operator can re-declare). New Hardware Setup → Stage **"Override / Sync Axis Position"** card (per-axis zero-ref readout + value spinbox in mm + "⟳ Set Position"). New `ZPStage.set_position(axis, value)` (generalization of `set_zero`: `G92 <phys><value>` via live `axis_map`); new `StageController.override_zp_position(axis, value)` sends `G92` setting the *physical* counter to `value + zero_position[axis]` so the zero-ref readout becomes `value` while the established zero reference is **preserved** (unlike Set Zero, which zeroes both); no motion. Also: `ZPStageSimulator` now honors `G92` (`_cmd_set_position`) — it was a silent no-op, so even Set Zero never updated the simulated M114 readout. **Addendum — last-known-position save + restore prompt:** `MainWindow.save_settings()` saves a `zp_last_position` snapshot (zero-ref mm per axis + ISO timestamp) on clean shutdown; new `StageController.on_connect` callback (mirrors `on_disconnect`, fired in `connect_stages`) → `app.py` `stage_connected` Signal → `_maybe_prompt_zp_position_restore` asks on first real ZP connect whether to assign the saved values to all axes (calls `override_zp_position` per axis; once/session; skipped in sim). New `StageController.get_zp_position_zero_ref()`. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_position_override.py` (14) + `tests/test_v75x_zp_position_restore.py` (7) |
| `MEBP_v75x_CAMERA_CAL_START_LIVEVIEW.md` | v7.5.x three camera-calibration fixes: (1) per-slot **Start/Stop** + collapsible live `CameraFeedView` preview on Hardware Setup → Cameras (*Detection & Assignment* rows) so the user can power a camera on and verify the feed on the calibration page itself (previously cameras only started inside modal dialogs); source combos now drive `mgr.set_source` (were display-only) so Start opens the *selected* source; state tracked via `camera_started`/`camera_stopped`. (2) **Needle-zero / plate-location µm/px bug** — `calibration.py::_needle_loc_camera_info` + plate edge-fit read `getattr(<CameraWidget>, "_um_per_px", 0.0)` (the widget never carries it → always 0.0 → *"camera µm/px or frame size not available"* even after needle-camera calibration); now read the shared `CameraManager.get_um_per_px`, gated on a new explicit `CameraManager.is_um_per_px_calibrated` flag (`_um_per_px_set`, set in `set_um_per_px`) so the *uncalibrated* case still fails cleanly instead of silently using the 1.67 seed default. (3) Needle Cameras Setup card shows "— (not calibrated)" instead of the bogus 1.67 seed. **Persistence (identity-keyed):** needle/plate µm/px saved in the hardware config via new `HardwareConfig.camera_calibrations` dict keyed by *device identity* — new `gui/widgets/camera_identity.py` reads the Windows DirectShow `FriendlyName`+`DevicePath` (model + USB port, via pygrabber/comtypes) so two identical cameras get distinct keys while still capturing the type (e.g. "Teslong Camera (port 6&29d1719c&2)"); `CameraManager.camera_identity` resolves a slot's id, `camera_widget` opens via `CAP_DSHOW` + labels with the friendly name, `set_calibrated_um_per_px` stores by id + saves, `_restore_calibration_for_slot` re-applies on source assignment. Calibration follows the physical camera+port across slot reassignment/restart; degrades to index-based id off-Windows; microscope still authoritative via objectives.json. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_cal_liveview.py` |
| `MEBP_v750_ZP_RECONNECT_HOTFIX.md` | v7.5.0 hotfix: ZP (Marlin Z+pumps) reconnect read **"Connected"** but was dead until a full app restart (XY/ProScan unaffected). Root cause asymmetry: `ZPStageManager._initialise_serial()` *returns None* (vs. `XYStage` which **raises**) when no Marlin is acquired, so `connect_stages` kept a `serial=None` object and `is_zp_connected` (`zp_stage is not None`) lied while every command no-oped in `send_data`. Fix layers in `StageController.py`: (1) post-construct serial-liveness check nulls the dead `zp_stage` + fires `on_disconnect` (mirrors XY) + hardened `is_zp_connected` (live-serial, sim short-circuits); (2) robust `disconnect_zp` — drop poller ZP ref *before* close, `remove_periodic` the leaked M500 cb, clear `_preferred_zp_port`, + deleted duplicate `disconnect_xy`/`disconnect_zp` defs; (3) `PositionPoller` poller-driven liveness (new `ZPStage._last_position_read_ok` since `get_current_position()` returns stale floats not None on a dead board) escalates ~2.5 s of failed reads → `_handle_disconnect("ZP")` (now `_disconnect_lock`-serialized + idempotent) to catch power-off-with-USB the port-handle watchdog can't see; (4) `gui/app.py` `stage_disconnected` Signal wires the never-assigned `on_disconnect` → immediate GUI refresh; (5) INFO-level probe diagnostics in `ZPStage._try_open_marlin` (open-fail OS error / M115-no-answer). Layer 6 (DTR-no-reset probe) deferred pending real-HW logs. Sim suite green (2 pre-existing state-persistence flakes) |
| `MEBP_v75x_WORKSPACE_ORIENTATION_AND_CLICK_FIX.md` | v7.5.x two hardware-surfaced fixes: (1) **workspace 180° display flip** — `JogWorkspaceView` drew +X→right/+Y→down but the Prior II stage origin is bottom-right with +X/+Y toward top-left, so the plate rendered upside-down; new `_FLIP_DISPLAY_180` constant + `_apply_display_flip()` reflects zero-ref µm about the envelope centre inside both `_um_to_px`/`_px_to_um` (whole render rotates, clicks stay exact). (2) **click-to-move 1000× overshoot** — workspace emits zero-ref µm but the three click/goto handlers (`jog_control._on_workspace_position_clicked`, `standard_jog_context._absolute_goto`, `spheroid_pickup_workflow._on_workspace_position_clicked`) passed µm into `move_xy_absolute(from_zero_ref=True)` which expects mm; now ÷1000 (safe_travel paths already used absolute µm, unchanged). Also fixed: added missing `StageController.move_xy_absolute_um()` (called in calibration auto-cal/ring-scan but never defined → AttributeError); takes absolute stage µm, clamps in zero-ref frame |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_CENTERING.md` | v7.5.x default well-plate centering in the XY safety envelope: the uncalibrated plate is centered on the envelope **midpoint** instead of `zero_position` (which only equaled the center for a symmetric envelope — asymmetric profiles like `ME3B V1` 0..114332/0..76645 put the plate at the corner). New `SafetyLimits.xy_center()` (zero-ref midpoint) + `StageController.default_plate_center_um()` (adds `zero_position` → absolute stage µm; centralizes the zero-ref↔absolute frame conversion; equals `zero_position` for the symmetric default = no regression). All 5 default-seed sites in `calibration.py` (`set_hardware_config`, `_wells_in_zero_ref` fallback [+frame-bug fix], scan/manual-fit seeds, simulated-camera anchor) + `jog_control.py::load_startup_plate` route through it. | `MEBP_v75x_AXIS_MAP_JOG_FIXES.md` | v7.5.x two-bug fix surfacing on ME3B V1's non-default `axis_map` (`Z→Z`, `P1→X`, …): (1) jog-Z direction/safety-bypass — `StageController.move_z_relative` clamp (+ `_calibrate_zero`, `reset_z_zero`, `wait_for_z_arrival`, `ZPJogHandler._jog_loop`, and `settings_page._set_z_from_current`) read logical Z from the hardcoded physical index 0 (the P1 pump under ME3B V1) instead of resolving via `_axis_index`/`_axis_letter`, so Z bypassed its soft-limit and could drive a constant direction; all now route through the live `axis_map` (strict no-op under the default map). (2) `HardwareControlPanel` jog speeds now seed to ½ of the calibrated max (`safety_limits.max_xy_speed` / `device_profile.per_axis_max_feedrate["Z"]` / `safety_limits.max_pump_feedrate`) and cap each spinbox at that max. ---

## Code Conventions

- **Theme**: Catppuccin Mocha dark (QSS generated by `gui/styles.py::build_theme(scale)`)
- **GUI Scaling**: All pixel dimensions through `gui/scaling.py::s()`, font sizes through `scaled_font_size()`. Never hardcode raw pixel values in GUI code.
- **Config format**: JSON everywhere (hardware, controllers, prints, settings)
- **Trajectories**: Nx7 numpy arrays `[x, y, z, p1, p2, p3, t]`
- **Serial**: pyserial RS-232, retry decorator with exponential backoff
- **Threading**: `Processor` command bus for thread safety; Qt signals for GUI updates
- **Testing**: unittest + physics-based simulators (`XYStageSimulator`, `ZPStageSimulator`, `SimulatedCamera`)
- **Vision**: OpenCV-based detection in `VisionDetector.py`; `DetectionWorker` QThread for parallel processing; `DetectionOverlay` for camera feed annotations
- **Executable**: PyInstaller via `python build_exe.py` — produces `dist/MEBP/` standalone folder
